# Marketing and Sales

[All categories](../README.md#categories) · 1,168 connectors · Last updated November 23, 2026 · [Star the list](https://github.com/rdmgator12/awesome-claude-connectors) if it helped

Connectors marked **`A`** are built and maintained by Anthropic; **`C`** marks the in-app catalog's Community badge (third-party built, not vetted like web-directory entries). Methodology and the held-entry policy are in the [README](../README.md) and [CONTRIBUTING.md](../CONTRIBUTING.md).

## Advertising and Paid Media

- [Ad Platform Updates](https://mcp-apps-muller.netlify.app/ad-updates/) **`C`** - Returns dated summaries of Meta and Google Ads changes from platform announcements, trade press and practitioner reports, each with a source link and verification level, refreshed daily. *Use case: Checking whether a newly announced Meta campaign feature has rolled out, finding what changed in Google Ads this week, looking for reports that explain a sudden cost jump.*
- [Ad Radar](https://ad-radar.dev) **`C`** - Searches a board of Meta (Facebook and Instagram) ads from DTC and e-commerce brands with at least $50k in estimated spend, reads full copy, transcripts and tags, shows which hooks, angles, formats and proof levels reach the top tier, profiles a brand's hook and angle mix, and generates a brief mapping a winning ad's structure onto a described product. *Use case: Finding top-spending skincare video ads that open with a problem hook, reviewing the hook mix a competitor brand is running, turning a winning ad into a creative brief for a new supplement.*
- [Ad Superpowers](https://adsuperpowers.ai) **`C`** - Reads and, with confirmation, writes across Meta, Google Ads, Analytics 4, Search Console, Tag Manager, Merchant Center, LinkedIn, and TikTok accounts, logging every action. *Use case: Answering one cross-channel performance question instead of opening eight dashboards, creating a new ad left paused for review, checking the audit log of recent changes.*
- [Ad Swipe File](https://adswipefile.com) **`C`** - Searches a curated library of real ads from brands on Meta, TikTok and other platforms by brand, niche and format, reads each ad's audiences and, on eligible plans, its copy, and lists saved and followed ads, with no spend or performance data. *Use case: Pulling recent UGC ads in the supplements niche, reading a skincare brand's testimonial ad copy, studying hooks before drafting new ad copy.*
- [AdAce Ads](https://adace.website) **`C`** - Reads live Google Ads and Meta performance, wasted spend, anomalies and cross-platform client overviews with GA4, Search Console and Klaviyo reports and a tracking audit, and proposes changes and creates paused campaigns, ad sets and ads that apply only after approval. *Use case: Finding search terms that spend without converting, proposing a budget shift between campaigns, creating a paused Meta campaign for review.*
- [Adako](https://adako.ai) **`C`** - Reads campaign performance in Google Ads, Meta Ads, LinkedIn Ads and ChatGPT Ads, proposes campaign changes for approval, approves or rejects pending proposals, and sets up account monitoring alerts. *Use case: Finding Google Ads search terms that spent money without converting last week, drafting a Meta spring sale campaign with a 40-dollar daily budget, alerting when LinkedIn cost per lead passes 60 dollars.*
- [AdBase](https://getadbase.com) **`C`** - Tracks competitors' Meta and Google ads, landing pages and email newsletters, searches and shows an ad library with creative tags and transcripts, and adds or removes watched advertisers. *Use case: Reviewing a competitor's longest-running Facebook ads, comparing landing page offers, summarizing what rivals promoted in newsletters this month.*
- [AdCrunch](https://adcrunch.dev) **`C`** - Reads ad performance and campaign structures across Meta, TikTok, Google Ads and OpenAI ad accounts, and on Meta creates, copies, renames, pauses and resumes campaigns, ad sets, creatives and ads and changes budgets, bids and schedules. *Use case: Comparing ROAS across Meta and TikTok for last week, pausing three Meta ads with CPMs above 30 dollars, duplicating a winning Meta ad set as a paused copy.*
- [AdKit - Ads Manager](https://adkit.so) **`C`** - Manage campaigns, ad sets, keywords, audiences, and budgets across Meta, Google, TikTok, LinkedIn, Reddit, Microsoft, and X Ads, with reporting on spend, ROAS, and cost per result, PPC budget audits, a library of 500k+ competitor ads, and changes that stay drafts until approved. *Use case: Pulling last week's ROAS before a client report, pausing a campaign whose cost per result crossed a threshold, checking which competitor creatives have run longest before briefing a new set.*
- [AdLabs - Amazon Ads](https://adlabs.app) **`C`** - Reports Amazon Sponsored Products, Brands, Display, and DSP performance at several levels, audits account health, and applies bulk bid and targeting changes. *Use case: Finding search terms burning spend without sales, running an account health scorecard before a client call, applying a bulk bid cut to weak keywords.*
- [Adlane](https://adlane.app) **`C`** - Read-only review of connected Google and Meta ad accounts, campaign configuration and performance across date ranges. *Use case: Comparing last month's Google and Meta campaign results, checking a campaign's settings, spotting accounts with rising costs.*
- [Adlicio](https://tryadlicio.com) **`C`** - Searches Reddit by topic, scrapes comments and reviews from supported public URLs, ranks the pains, objections, hooks and quotes that repeat, researches public competitor ads and short-form videos, and saves scripts to a brand workspace, with the full toolset on the All Access plan. *Use case: Pulling a recurring complaint from product reviews to find a new ad hook, turning a glowing customer quote into ad copy for a new campaign.*
- [AdLigner](https://adligner.com) **`C`** - Analyzes and manages Google, Microsoft, Meta, TikTok, LinkedIn, Reddit and ChatGPT ad accounts alongside GA4 and Klaviyo, finding wasted spend, pacing and creative fatigue, and creating, updating, pausing or resuming campaigns, ad groups, ads, audiences, experiments, automation rules, negative keywords and Klaviyo campaigns, with every change previewed and run only after confirmation. *Use case: Finding wasted spend across Google search terms, comparing Meta conversions with GA4, pausing fatigued TikTok creatives.*
- [AdLupe](https://adlupe.com) **`C`** - Tracks competitors' ads from the public EU ad libraries of Meta and Google, labeling each as new, test, winner or steady, shows daily changes, compares competitors, searches ads and industry overviews, and adds or removes tracked competitors. *Use case: Checking which new ads a rival launched since yesterday, listing a competitor's ads running 30 days or more, comparing two competitors side by side.*
- [AdMan mcp](https://ad-man.io) **`C`** - Reads marketplace accounts, sales, ad campaign and ad group metrics, category performance and automation alerts in AdMan, and updates campaign budgets, status and ROAS targets, ad group rules and status, and moves ad groups between campaigns. *Use case: Pausing ad groups with negative margin on a marketplace account, raising the daily budget of a best-selling campaign, checking which categories lost profit last month.*
- [Admiral](https://meetadmiral.com) **`C`** - Reads Google Ads and Meta ad account data, including campaigns, costs, keywords, search terms, ads, alerts and trends, and files suggested changes such as pausing keywords or adding negatives into an approval queue without editing the ad platforms directly. *Use case: Finding keywords that spent money with no conversions last month, reviewing weekly cost trends, proposing a negative keyword for approval.*
- [Adology](https://adologyai.com) **`C`** - Competitive ad intelligence for marketers that pulls a brand's ad creative, hooks, and spend patterns across Meta, TikTok, YouTube, and more. *Use case: Checking a competitor's TikTok ad spend before planning next quarter's budget, pulling a rival's best-performing ad hooks on Meta for inspiration.*
- [Adomik Jean Pierre](https://www.adomik.com) **`C`** - Analyzes advertising and monetization data across sources, benchmarks performance against the market, and produces reports, forecasts, and summary decks. *Use case: Benchmarking yield against market rates, investigating an anomaly in yesterday's revenue, assembling a quarterly wrap-up deck for a publisher.*
- [adplane](https://adplane.ai) **`C`** - Google Ads and Meta Ads reporting and campaign management covering search terms, spend comparisons, GAQL queries, and creation of paused campaigns, ad groups, and creatives. *Use case: Comparing Google and Meta spend over the same date range, pulling search terms that wasted budget, building a paused search campaign for review before it runs.*
- [Ads MCP Audit](https://adsmcp.dev) **`C`** - Audits Google Ads accounts read-only for wasted spend, generates negative keyword and pause CSV files to import, compares results before and after a change, writes client reports, and runs read-only GAQL queries. *Use case: Finding search terms that spent budget without converting last month, exporting a negative keyword CSV for Google Ads Editor, checking whether a bid change lowered cost per conversion.*
- [Adsby MCP](https://adsby.co) **`C`** - Reports Google Ads campaign, ad group, keyword and search-term metrics, Meta Ads insights by account, campaign, ad set or ad with demographic, country and placement breakdowns, and Google Analytics traffic, acquisition, events and ecommerce purchases for accounts connected in an Adsby workspace. *Use case: Finding search terms with high cost and no conversions, breaking Meta purchase ROAS down by age and placement, checking which channels drove purchases last month.*
- [Adshelf](https://adshelf.app) **`C`** - Analyzes competitors' Facebook and Instagram ads archived from Meta's Ad Library, surfacing long-running creatives, hooks, angles, ad copy, and category benchmarks. *Use case: Breaking down a rival brand's creative strategy, pulling the hooks behind its longest-running ads, comparing a category's ad longevity before a launch.*
- [AdShields](https://www.adshields.pro/en) **`C`** - Creative operations hub: a library of videos and VSLs where your team writes scripts, comments by passage, versions, approves, and pushes to your ad platform — all in natural language. *Use case: Leaving a comment on a specific line of a VSL script before recording, approving a final video cut before it pushes live to the ad platform.*
- [Adside — Meta Ads, Google Ads & LinkedIn Ads reporting for agencies](https://adside.ai) **`C`** - Reads Meta, Google, and LinkedIn Ads performance data alongside a brand kit, tracked competitors, and saved team knowledge for reporting and on-brand copy. *Use case: Comparing cost per result across Meta and Google for last month, drafting on-brand ad headlines, reviewing what tracked competitors are running.*
- [Adsmom](https://adsmom.com) **`C`** - Searches competitor ads across Meta, TikTok, Google, YouTube, and LinkedIn, plus organic TikTok and Instagram content, with reach, estimated spend, and targeting data. *Use case: Reviewing a competitor's active creatives, estimating a rival brand's ad spend, finding which organic TikTok formats are gaining reach.*
- [Adspirer Ads & Performance Marketing Agent](https://www.adspirer.com) - Your performance marketing and paid media agent — an Agent that plans, launches, and autonomously runs your ad campaigns across Google, Meta, LinkedIn, TikTok, and more. *Use case: Launching a lead-gen campaign on LinkedIn before a product launch, shifting budget from an underperforming TikTok ad to a stronger one on Meta.*
- [Adszy](https://adszy.ai) **`C`** - Analyzes linked Google Ads accounts, covering performance comparisons, search terms, negative-keyword candidates, wasted spend, budget pacing, anomalies, auction insights, products, feed issues and change history, and on paid Adszy Connect plans prepares bid, budget, status and campaign changes held for approval. *Use case: Finding the search terms that wasted spend last month, explaining why costs jumped last week.*
- [AdvisorPPC - Google Ads, Analytics, Tag Manager, Search Console and YouTube Connector](https://advisorppc.com) **`C`** - Reads and updates Google Ads, Google Analytics 4, Google Tag Manager, Google Search Console and YouTube accounts the person authorizes, with 33 reads and 13 limited writes such as negative keywords, CPC bids, tags and playlists, each previewed first and applied only when confirmed. *Use case: Checking how a Google marketing campaign performed this week, pulling a quick report before a stakeholder meeting.*
- [AdWell](https://runadwell.com) **`C`** - Reads Apple Search Ads keywords, search terms, spend and subscription revenue in Adwell, lists proposals and Apple recommendations and drafts changes. *Use case: Finding keywords that spent money last month without bringing a paid subscriber.*
- [AdWhispr Ads](https://adwhispr.com) - Searches brands' live Meta and TikTok ad libraries, builds competitive briefs, and launches, adjusts, and reports on Google, Meta, and TikTok campaigns. *Use case: Pulling a competitor's longest-running hooks, generating a positioning brief on a rival brand, launching a paused search campaign for review before it spends.*
- [Adzviser AdRoll](https://adzviser.com/mcp/adroll) **`C`** - Reads AdRoll reporting data through Adzviser, covering impressions, clicks, spend and conversions, and compares campaigns, ad groups and creatives over time. *Use case: Comparing retargeting campaign conversions month over month, finding the AdRoll creative with the lowest cost per conversion.*
- [Adzviser Amazon Ads](https://adzviser.com/mcp/amazon-ads) **`C`** - Reads Amazon Ads reporting data through Adzviser, covering spend, sales and ACoS, and compares campaigns, keywords and product targets over time. *Use case: Finding keywords with ACoS above 40 percent last month, comparing Sponsored Products spend before and after Prime Day.*
- [Adzviser Amazon DSP](https://adzviser.com/mcp/amazon-dsp) **`C`** - Reads Amazon DSP reporting data through Adzviser, covering impressions, detail page views and return on ad spend, and compares orders, line items, creatives and audiences over time. *Use case: Comparing ROAS across three DSP line items for the holiday flight, checking which audience drove the most detail page views.*
- [Adzviser Apple Ads](https://adzviser.com/mcp/apple-ads) **`C`** - Reads Apple Ads reporting data through Adzviser, covering impressions, taps, installs and spend, and compares campaigns, ad groups and keywords over time. *Use case: Finding keywords with the lowest cost per install last week, comparing installs from two campaigns.*
- [Adzviser Bing Ads](https://adzviser.com/mcp/microsoft-ads) **`C`** - Reads Microsoft Advertising reporting data through Adzviser, covering spend, conversions and ROAS, and compares campaigns and keyword performance over time. *Use case: Checking which Bing campaigns lost conversions week over week, finding keywords that spent 200 dollars without a sale.*
- [Adzviser CM360](https://adzviser.com/mcp/campaign-manager-360) **`C`** - Reads Campaign Manager 360 reporting data through Adzviser, covering impressions, clicks and conversions, and compares campaigns, sites and placements over time. *Use case: Ranking publisher sites by conversions for a spring campaign, checking which placements underdelivered impressions last week.*
- [Adzviser Criteo](https://adzviser.com/mcp/criteo) **`C`** - Reads Criteo reporting data through Adzviser, covering cost, revenue and ROAS, and compares campaigns, ad sets, ads, categories and channels over time. *Use case: Comparing ROAS of two Criteo retargeting ad sets for November, finding the product category with the highest revenue.*
- [Adzviser DV360](https://adzviser.com/mcp/display-video-360) **`C`** - Reads Display and Video 360 reporting data through Adzviser, covering impressions, clicks and revenue, and compares campaigns, insertion orders and line items over time. *Use case: Finding line items with falling click-through rates this month, comparing revenue across two insertion orders.*
- [Adzviser Google Ads](https://adzviser.com/mcp/google-ads) **`C`** - Reads Google Ads reporting data through Adzviser, covering spend, conversions and ROAS, and compares campaigns and keyword performance over time. *Use case: Checking why cost per conversion rose last week, comparing brand and non-brand campaign ROAS for Q3.*
- [Adzviser Instacart](https://adzviser.com/mcp/instacart) **`C`** - Reads Instacart Ads reporting data through Adzviser, covering spend, attributed sales and ROAS, and compares campaigns, ad groups and sponsored product, display and video performance over time. *Use case: Comparing ROAS of two snack brand campaigns for October, checking whether video ads drove more attributed sales than display.*
- [Adzviser LinkedIn Ads](https://adzviser.com/mcp/linkedin-ads) **`C`** - Reads LinkedIn Ads reporting data through Adzviser, covering spend, leads and cost per lead, and compares campaigns, audiences and creatives over time. *Use case: Finding the LinkedIn campaign with the lowest cost per lead in September, comparing two job-title audiences.*
- [Adzviser Meta Ads](https://adzviser.com/mcp/meta-ads) **`C`** - Reads Meta Ads reporting data for Facebook and Instagram through Adzviser, covering spend, conversions and ROAS, and compares campaigns, ad sets and creatives over time. *Use case: Finding the ad set with the best ROAS last week, checking whether a new creative lowered cost per purchase.*
- [Adzviser MNTN](https://adzviser.com/mcp/mntn) **`C`** - Reads MNTN connected TV reporting data through Adzviser, covering impressions, visits and conversions, and compares campaigns, creatives and audiences over time. *Use case: Comparing site visits from two CTV creatives in October, checking conversions by audience segment.*
- [Adzviser Pinterest Ads](https://adzviser.com/mcp/pinterest-ads) **`C`** - Reads Pinterest Ads reporting data through Adzviser, covering spend, impressions and conversions, and compares campaigns, ad groups, creatives and audience targeting over time. *Use case: Finding the pin creative with the most conversions for a home decor campaign, comparing spend across two ad groups.*
- [Adzviser Reddit Ads](https://adzviser.com/mcp/reddit-ads) **`C`** - Reads Reddit Ads reporting data through Adzviser, covering spend, conversions and ROAS, and compares campaigns, ad groups and community targeting over time. *Use case: Comparing conversions from two subreddit targeting setups, checking Reddit ROAS for last month.*
- [Adzviser SA360](https://adzviser.com/mcp/search-ads-360) **`C`** - Reads Search Ads 360 reporting data through Adzviser, covering impressions, clicks, cost and conversions, and compares campaigns, ad groups and keywords over time. *Use case: Comparing cost per conversion across campaigns last week, finding keywords that lost clicks month over month.*
- [Adzviser Snapchat Ads](https://adzviser.com/mcp/snapchat-ads) **`C`** - Reads Snapchat Ads reporting data through Adzviser, covering impressions, cost and leads, and compares campaigns, ad types and regions over time. *Use case: Comparing cost per lead in the UK and Germany last month, checking which ad type drove the most leads.*
- [Adzviser Spotify Ads](https://adzviser.com/mcp/spotify-ads) **`C`** - Reads Spotify Ads reporting data through Adzviser, covering impressions, clicks, spend and listens, and compares campaigns, ad sets and ad formats over time. *Use case: Comparing audio and video ad clicks for a podcast launch, checking weekly spend on a summer campaign.*
- [Adzviser The Trade Desk](https://adzviser.com/mcp/the-trade-desk) **`C`** - Reads The Trade Desk reporting data through Adzviser, covering impressions, spend and conversions, and compares campaigns, ad groups and creatives over time. *Use case: Finding ad groups with rising CPMs this month, comparing conversions from two programmatic creatives.*
- [Adzviser TikTok Ads](https://adzviser.com/mcp/tiktok-ads) **`C`** - Reads TikTok Ads reporting data through Adzviser, covering spend, conversions and ROAS, and compares campaigns, ad groups and creatives over time. *Use case: Finding TikTok creatives with falling ROAS this week, comparing spend across two prospecting campaigns.*
- [Adzviser Vibe.co](https://adzviser.com/mcp/vibe) **`C`** - Reads Vibe.co connected TV reporting data through Adzviser, covering spend, households reached and completed views, and compares campaigns, creatives, channels and regions over time. *Use case: Comparing completed views of two CTV spots in Texas, checking households reached by a holiday campaign.*
- [Adzviser X Ads](https://adzviser.com/mcp/x-ads) **`C`** - Reads X Ads reporting data through Adzviser, covering spend, impressions and conversions, and compares campaigns, line items, creatives and audience targeting over time. *Use case: Comparing conversions from two promoted post creatives, checking X Ads spend for the week of a product launch.*
- [Agency AI](https://www.agencyai.app) **`C`** - Meta and Google ad intelligence for Shopify stores, covering performance analysis, optimal architecture, budget strategy, and creative direction. *Use case: Checking why a Shopify store's Meta ad spend spiked this week, getting a budget recommendation before scaling up a Google campaign.*
- [Agency Autopilot](https://getagencyautopilot.com) **`C`** - Connects an agency's client Google Ads, Analytics 4, Search Console, Business Profile, Meta ads, WordPress and HighLevel CRM accounts to run reports, build branded client reports, manage campaigns, keywords, budgets and bidding, reply to reviews, post to Business Profiles, draft site pages and handle CRM leads, with changes previewed before confirmation. *Use case: Finding wasted spend and adding negative keywords for a client, building a monthly cross-channel report as a branded page, replying to new Business Profile reviews.*
- [Alison.AI](https://alison.ai) **`C`** - Analyzes ad creative performance across Meta, Google, TikTok, and other channels at the element level: hooks, colors, messaging, and pacing. *Use case: Finding which hook styles lifted click-through this month, spotting ads showing fatigue before spend is wasted, scoring a new creative ahead of launch.*
- [AMZ Vault](https://www.amz-vault.com) **`C`** - Reports Amazon settlement-based profit and margin per product and manages Sponsored Products, Brands, and Display campaigns, staging every change for explicit approval. *Use case: Checking margin after fees on last month's top-selling product, staging negative keywords for search terms that spent without orders, approving a staged bid change.*
- [Appier Ad Cloud Portal](https://www.appier.com/en/ad-cloud) **`C`** - Reads Appier Ad Cloud Portal campaign and creative reports by date for the accounts a user can access, and lists available metrics and report accounts. *Use case: Comparing last week's campaign spend and conversions with the week before, finding the creative with the highest click rate in October.*
- [Ativa Dash](https://ativadash.com) **`C`** - Reads Meta Ads and Google Ads spend, leads, CPL, CPA and ROAS by client, campaign, ad set and ad, compares periods, analyzes creatives and form leads and diagnoses broken UTMs, and with admin permission creates paused campaigns, edits ads, pauses weak campaigns, scales winners and adjusts budgets after confirmation. *Use case: Comparing a client's cost per lead with last month, finding fatigued creatives, pausing underperforming Meta campaigns.*
- [Audienzes](https://audienzes.com) **`C`** - Reads live ad account and campaign data, including status, impressions, clicks, spend and daily trends, across Meta, TikTok, LinkedIn, Pinterest, Snapchat, Google Ads, Reddit, Spotify, Amazon Ads and X Ads. *Use case: Comparing spend across TikTok and Snapchat campaigns this week, checking which LinkedIn campaigns are paused, charting daily clicks for a Pinterest campaign.*
- [Autron](https://autron.ai) **`C`** - Reports ACoS, TACoS, spend, sales and profit by campaign, search term or product from connected Amazon Ads and Seller Central accounts, and on Agent and Pro plans changes bids, budgets, targets and campaigns, asking before each change and recording it in an action log. *Use case: Changing an underperforming bid, checking which product ad drove the most sales, adjusting budget on a campaign that's overspending.*
- [BidX - Scale Marketplace Ads](https://bidx.io) **`C`** - Queries Amazon Ads data from a BidX account for audits, charts, budget recommendations, and own-brand or competitor ASIN tracking. *Use case: Auditing last month's ad spend for wasted budget, charting ACoS by campaign, watching a competitor ASIN ahead of a product launch.*
- [Birch Production](https://bir.ch) **`C`** - Audits, debugs and manages ad automation rules for Meta, Google, TikTok and Snapchat Ads in plain language. *Use case: Finding why a pause rule didn't fire, reviewing the rules guarding CPA, creating a rule that scales winning ad sets.*
- [Camberstack](https://camberstack.io) **`C`** - Reads Google Ads accounts with GAQL queries, keyword ideas and metrics, and Search Console data, proposes changes to keywords, budgets, bids, targeting, goals and new campaigns, and applies or undoes approved changes with a change history. *Use case: Finding search terms wasting spend, proposing negative keywords and a budget shift, building a paused Performance Max campaign for review.*
- [CETV Now! MCP](https://cetvnow.com) **`C`** - Lists CETV Now digital screen ad packages with price, plays and duration, creates upload links for an ad image, creates campaigns after confirming the details, and reads campaign reports and invoice links. *Use case: Launching a two-week break room screen campaign for a local gym with an existing 1920 by 1080 ad, checking plays delivered so far.*
- [Clarmix](https://clarmix.com) **`C`** - Analyzes Google Ads, Google Merchant Center and Shopify data, including campaign and product performance, ROAS, search terms, PMax structure, feed issues, labels, inventory and profitability, and runs GAQL queries. *Use case: Finding Shopping products with high spend and no conversions last month, checking which Merchant Center items are disapproved, comparing margins by product label.*
- [Converly](https://converly.io) **`C`** - Configures server-side conversion tracking to Google Ads, Meta, LinkedIn, TikTok, Microsoft Ads, and GA4, with conditional firing rules and a debug log. *Use case: Setting up a conversion that fires only on quote-request submissions, generating the snippet to paste on a site, checking the log to confirm an event reached Google Ads.*
- [Creativity](https://www.creativity.run) **`C`** - Reads Meta ad account performance, creatives, AI tags, transcripts and comments in a creative-analytics workspace, analyzes tested hooks and angles, runs saved reports and creates, updates, deletes and restores reports when edits are allowed, asking first unless that tool is set to always allow. *Use case: Finding the five creatives with the highest ROAS last month, checking which hook angles have not been tested.*
- [crm.care](https://flowolf.ai) **`C`** - Turns a brief into a draft B2B campaign in a connected workspace, then reports campaign status, CRM sync state, and goal-level pipeline and spend. *Use case: Drafting a campaign from a one-paragraph brief, checking which campaigns are live and synced to the CRM, reviewing pacing against budget before committing more.*
- [Daily Command](https://doceree.com/daily-command) **`C`** - Continuously observes your brands, campaigns, budgets, and market signals - then recommends, routes approval, executes, and measures the next best move. *Use case: Flagging a budget overspend before it becomes a problem, routing a recommended bid change for approval, measuring whether last week's move actually worked.*
- [DataFuel](https://datafuel.tech) **`C`** - Reports Amazon advertising performance by account, campaign, ad group, keyword, search term, placement, audience, day-parting and product, with daily trends, keyword rank, share of voice and change history, and creates campaigns, ad groups and portfolios, adds or negates keywords and search terms, and updates bids, budgets, placement bids and status. *Use case: Finding search terms that spent without converting and negating them, raising budgets on campaigns that run out by mid-afternoon, checking keyword rank trends for a top-selling product.*
- [Enriq.ai: Google Ads, Meta Ads & TikTok Ads Analyst](https://enriq.ai) **`C`** - Analyzes connected Google Ads, Meta Ads, TikTok Ads, LinkedIn Ads and Snapchat Ads accounts read-only, audits Performance Max, Advantage+ and Quality Score, checks conversion tracking, finds negative keywords and runs saved analysis routines. *Use case: Explaining why CPA jumped 30 percent on a Meta campaign last week, finding wasted search terms in a Google Ads account before a monthly client report.*
- [Epsilo](https://epsilo.ai) **`C`** - Queries retail media advertising data across Shopee, Lazada, TikTok Shop and other networks, covering ad account spend, ROAS and campaigns with status and budget, explains metric definitions and searches product help docs, read-only. *Use case: Ranking ad accounts by spend and ROAS last week, comparing Shopee and Lazada performance, looking up how a metric is calculated.*
- [Equativ Maestro](https://equativ.com) **`C`** - Searches, creates and updates Equativ Maestro deals, advertisers, campaigns, line items, creatives, budgets and site lists, troubleshoots delivery and pacing, and runs performance reports. *Use case: Creating a curated deal with inventory targeting, troubleshooting an underdelivering line item, pulling last week's campaign metrics.*
- [FLAGTRAIL](https://flagtrail.com) **`C`** - Reads paid media accounts running on Google Ads, Meta Ads, TikTok and Unity Ads, covering off-target campaigns, budget spent against plan, daily spend, clicks, impressions and conversions by platform, open alerts and their rules, campaign change history with before-and-after results, creative performance, media plans, reminders and connection status, in Brazilian Portuguese. *Use case: Finding which campaigns are behind on this month's budget, reviewing why a Meta campaign was changed and how results moved after, comparing creative performance on TikTok and Google Ads.*
- [Flowboost](https://flowboost.com) **`C`** - Analyzes Google Ads accounts through Wolfy, manages Flowboost projects, reports, schedules, templates, automations and Labelizer product labelling, and connects Google Ads accounts, saving settings as drafts and asking before anything goes live, such as enabling an automation, report schedule or Labelizer. *Use case: Asking Wolfy why CPA rose on a Shopping campaign last week, scheduling a monthly PDF report for a client.*
- [FRAMEN](https://www.framen.com) **`C`** - Book, launch, and manage digital out-of-home ad campaigns across FRAMEN's global screen network - straight from chat. *Use case: Booking a digital out-of-home campaign for a weekend promotion, checking which screens in the network are running it, pausing an underperforming campaign.*

- [WASK - Facebook & Google Ads](https://www.wask.co) **`C`** - Reports Google Ads and Meta Ads performance by account, campaign, ad group, ad, keyword, search term and creative, compares periods, identifies top and bottom performers, and previews and applies actions on campaigns, ad groups, ads and keywords. *Use case: Comparing last week's ROAS against the prior week, finding underperforming keywords and pausing them after a preview.*
- [Watching That](https://watchingthat.com) **`C`** - Ad stack data covering yield insights, revenue performance, and delivery issues. *Use case: Monitoring video ad revenue, troubleshooting fill-rate issues, tracking yield performance.*
- [Wexlyn](https://wexlyn.ai) **`C`** - Reports ad cost, revenue, profit and ROI by source, campaign or ad under several attribution models, reads lead journeys, sales and calls, and records sales, calls, leads and lead stages. *Use case: Finding which ad sets made money last month, reading a lead's full journey, recording a closed sale from a call.*
- [Windsor.ai for Google Ads](https://windsor.ai/connectors/google-ads) **`C`** - Connect Google Ads to Claude: query ad spend, ROAS, CPC, conversions, and keyword performance in plain language, then create or pause campaigns, adjust budgets, and manage keywords. *Use case: Pausing a campaign that is overspending, raising the budget on one with strong ROAS, pausing a keyword with high CPC and no conversions.*
- [Windsor.ai for LinkedIn Ads](https://windsor.ai/connectors/linkedin-ads) **`C`** - Connect LinkedIn Ads to Claude: query ad spend, impressions, clicks, leads, and conversions in plain language, then pause or enable campaign groups and campaigns or set budgets. *Use case: Pausing a campaign group that stopped generating leads, checking cost per conversion before increasing a budget, reviewing clicks on a new ad.*
- [Windsor.ai for Microsoft Ads (Bing)](https://windsor.ai/connectors/bing-ads) **`C`** - Connect Microsoft Ads (Bing) to Claude: query ad spend, impressions, clicks, conversions, and CPC in plain language, then pause or enable campaigns and ad groups or set budgets. *Use case: Pausing an ad group with a high CPC and no conversions, raising the budget on a campaign that is converting well, checking impressions before a launch.*
- [Windsor.ai for TikTok Ads](https://windsor.ai/connectors/tiktok-ads) **`C`** - Connect TikTok Ads to Claude: query ad spend, ROAS, conversions, and campaign performance in plain language, then pause campaigns or adjust budgets. *Use case: Pausing a campaign whose ROAS dropped overnight, checking conversions on a new video ad, adjusting a budget before the weekend.*
- [Yirla](https://www.yirla.com) **`C`** - Competitor ad intelligence and campaign analytics for LinkedIn, Google, YouTube, Meta, Reddit, and TikTok ads. *Use case: Checking which of a competitor's TikTok ads has run longest, comparing their LinkedIn campaign volume against last quarter, pulling their top-performing Meta creative.*
- [Yolodex](https://www.yolodex.ai) **`C`** - Ranks ads from a connected Meta ad account against typical ads at the same frequency and funnel stage, breaks down their hooks and formats, searches competitor ads and how long each ran, mines reviews, Reddit, Instagram and TikTok for customer language and writes creative briefs. *Use case: Checking which ad creative a competitor has been running the longest before launching a new campaign.*

## Brand Management and Content Creation

- [Abency](https://abency.com) - Manages an agency's brands, creating, scheduling, updating and deleting social posts for Facebook, Instagram, LinkedIn, TikTok and YouTube, running keyword research and SEO tracking, querying Google Search Console and analyzing Google Ads and Meta Ads campaigns. *Use case: Reviewing a brand's campaign status before a client meeting, comparing the performance of two brands in the agency's portfolio.*
- [aclipp](https://www.aclipp.com) **`C`** - Analyzes media coverage, reach and media value by period, outlet, channel, country and sentiment, tracks brand share of voice and cited sources in AI answers, compares competitors, adds articles by URL, and builds PR reports and dashboards. *Use case: Measuring last quarter's coverage reach for a product launch, comparing a client's AI-answer share of voice with two competitors, adding a new article to a project before a monthly report.*
- [AgentFabrica](https://agentfabrica.com) **`C`** - Reads a marketing operations panel's brands and tone of voice, content calendar, pending approvals, ad pre-test results, lead counts, WhatsApp line status, plan quota and generated content, and adds draft posts to a brand's approval queue without publishing, approving or deleting anything. *Use case: Reviewing which posts are waiting for approval this week, summarizing objections from the latest ad pre-test, queuing a draft product-launch post for the team to approve.*
- [Agility PR Solutions](https://www.agilitypr.com) **`C`** - Searches media coverage across online, print, broadcast, podcast, radio, and social sources, with sentiment, share of voice, and coverage trends. *Use case: Summarizing coverage of a product announcement, comparing sentiment against a competitor over a quarter, charting share of voice through a campaign window.*
- [Aivy](https://aivymarketing.com) **`C`** - Sets up small business marketing in Aivy, scanning a website for Brand DNA, building a strategy and a 12-month content plan, and drafting, scheduling, publishing and tracking posts on connected social channels, asking before each credit-using scan, strategy or plan and, with approval on, holding every post for review in Aivy. *Use case: Building a content plan for a Prague yoga studio from its website, scheduling three LinkedIn posts for next week.*
- [Anyword - Copy Performance](https://www.anyword.com/mcp) **`C`** - Scores ads, emails, social posts, landing pages and other marketing copy from 0 to 100 with Anyword's performance model, explains the score, returns improved scored versions and uses brand voices, playbooks and templates. *Use case: Scoring three subject lines for a spring sale email, rewriting a product description in the brand voice, writing an ad from a launch template.*
- [appfollow](https://appfollow.io) **`C`** - Reads ratings, reviews, AI review summaries, rankings and ASO keyword data for apps on App Store, Google Play, Trustpilot, Steam and other stores, filters reviews by country, version, language, rating and date, replies to reviews and adds apps and keywords. *Use case: Pulling data from a connected service into a conversation for a quick lookup, instead of switching to a separate app.*
- [AtmoScreen](https://atmoscreen.com) **`C`** - Lists waiting-room and lobby screens and their status, reads and creates slide programmes with weather, messages, QR codes and quizzes, and assigns programmes to one or more screens. *Use case: Creating an October waiting-room programme with local weather and a flu vaccination reminder, putting a new welcome loop on the reception screen, checking which screens are offline.*
- [Audesti - Voice testimonials](https://audesti.com) **`C`** - Reads voice testimonials with transcripts, sentiment and quotable phrases, creates testimonial requests and share links, approves and updates testimonials, and manages a share queue. *Use case: Pulling power phrases for a landing page, requesting testimonials from five recent customers, lining up the strongest testimonial to post.*

- [Black Ice](https://black-ice.ai) **`C`** - Looks up approved terminology per locale with allowed and forbidden variants, scans draft translations for terminology violations, returns market profiles with tone and locale guidance, checks market availability, browses the concept ontology and submits new term candidates to a human review queue. *Use case: Validating a Japanese product-page translation against approved terms, checking whether a feature is available in Brazil, proposing a new glossary term for review.*

- [qrlia](https://qrlia.com) **`C`** - Creates, updates and deletes dynamic QR codes for links, vCards, Wi-Fi, menus, PDFs, lead forms and digital product passports, returns PNG or SVG images, organizes codes in folders, templates and designs, and reports scan analytics. *Use case: Making a Wi-Fi code for hotel room cards, pointing a printed menu code at a new PDF, comparing scan counts across a campaign's codes.*
- [Quickesign](https://quickesign.net) **`C`** - Designs digital signage layouts such as menu boards and price lists, checks text fit and contrast with previews, uploads media, and assigns, schedules and refreshes content on linked screens. *Use case: Building a vertical salon price list for a window TV, scheduling lunch specials from 11 to 2, copying a menu board for a second location.*
- [Quotient](https://www.getquotient.ai) **`C`** - Plans and executes marketing campaigns across email, social, blog, and events, producing briefs, tasks, timelines, and the deliverables themselves. *Use case: Building a feature-launch campaign from a one-line goal, drafting the social posts for each channel, scheduling a webinar's supporting email sequence.*
- [Reach](https://reach.locus-intelligence.com) **`C`** - Reads and manages a Google Business Profile through Reach, including profile details, reviews and replies, posts, photos, performance, keyword rankings, competitors and leads, drafts review replies, and only with approval publishes replies and posts, updates supported profile fields and manages photos. *Use case: Replying to new reviews this week, publishing a holiday hours post.*
- [Reachype](https://reachype.com) **`C`** - Reads a marketing workspace's brand profile, content calendar, idea backlog, campaigns, products, research reports and performance dashboards, and rewrites the copy of planned posts or ideas with undo. *Use case: Reviewing next week's content calendar against the brand voice, rewriting a planned post's caption, checking which channels performed well last month.*
- [Rebrandly](https://www.rebrandly.com) **`C`** - Create and manage branded short links on your own domain, then track how they perform, without leaving Claude. *Use case: Creating a branded short link for a new campaign on a company domain, checking how many clicks a link got last week.*
- [Repuso](https://repuso.com/developers) **`C`** - Fetches reviews from 50+ platforms with sentiment labels and translation, tracks rating history, compares monitored competitor profiles, tags reviews, and manages webhooks. *Use case: Checking what changed after a bad review week, comparing a location's rating against a competitor, tagging complaints that mention delivery times.*
- [ReputeMap](https://reputemap.com) **`C`** - Lists Google reviews and review statistics across multi-location businesses, drafts replies in each business's saved voice, publishes approved replies to Google and sends review requests. *Use case: Finding locations with unanswered 1–3 star reviews from last week, drafting a reply in the reviewer's language and publishing it after approval.*
- [ReviewMix](https://reviewmix.eu) **`C`** - Reads Google reviews, testimonials and private feedback for businesses in ReviewMix on a Pro, Agency or Studio plan or trial, summarizes reviews and, when that permission is on, posts, replaces or deletes Google review replies, asking before each reply unless set to always allow. *Use case: Finding reviews from the last two weeks with no reply and answering them.*
- [Riddle MCP](https://www.riddle.com) **`C`** - Builds, edits, publishes and organizes Riddle interactive content — quizzes, polls, personality tests, forms, predictors, minigames and stories — and reports engagement statistics. *Use case: Building a product-match quiz from a brief, finding the question where respondents drop off, republishing a poll after rewriting its options.*
- [SagaJoe](https://sagajoe.com) **`C`** - Returns SagaJoe client briefs with sourced and dated facts on offers, pricing, audience, voice and rules, searches facts and proposes new ones for approval. *Use case: Pulling a client's current pricing and voice rules before drafting a landing page.*
- [Screens.icu](https://screens.icu) **`C`** - Builds slideshows and directory menus, assigns them to paired screens, and shows what each digital signage display is currently showing. *Use case: Adding slides to a lobby display, pointing a screen at a new directory menu, checking what every screen is showing.*
- [Seyclops](https://seyclops.com) **`C`** - Reads Google reviews, reputation scores and review-request results for local service businesses, lists reviews still needing an answer, drafts replies saved for owner approval, and prepares review requests for named customers that wait in Seyclops for approval. *Use case: Finding unanswered one-star reviews from this month, drafting a reply to a review about a late plumbing visit, preparing review requests for three recent HVAC customers.*
- [Sharebird Coach](https://sharebird.com) **`C`** - Reviews product marketing drafts against answers from more than 500 product marketing leaders, and provides searchable expert guidance and templates. *Use case: Checking a launch plan for gaps before a review, seeing how experienced marketers position a product with two buyers, pulling a competitive battlecard template.*
- [Shipstar](https://shipstar.ai) **`C`** - Generates changelogs, blog posts, release notes emails, and social posts from a project's GitHub commits, then revises, approves, publishes, and emails them. *Use case: Turning the last two weeks of commits into a changelog, revising a draft release email, sending release notes to a project mailing list.*
- [Sparkier](https://www.sparkier.io) **`C`** - Lists, creates, updates and publishes short paid consulting sessions on a freelance consulting platform, drafts and publishes consultant profile changes, drafts, schedules and publishes LinkedIn posts, drafts, publishes, unpublishes and archives articles, and reads consultation deliverables. *Use case: Drafting a LinkedIn post announcing a new AI strategy session and scheduling it for Tuesday at 9am, listing which sessions are published, summarizing actions from recent deliverables.*
- [SpeciAI Marketing](https://studio.speciai.team) **`C`** - Discovers topics from Korean statute amendments, rulings and news, drafts card-news carousels and blog posts with legal citations verified, renders and schedules publications, publishing or deleting only after a preview and explicit confirmation, and reads content metrics. *Use case: Drafting a card-news carousel on a new lease law for a law firm, scheduling a blog post for Monday morning.*
- [StartupSprints](https://startupsprints.com) **`C`** - Reviews a startup's saved profile, priorities and distribution plan, reads execution briefs, saves research, drafts and Distribution Kit content, records measured experiment outcomes, and submits approved directory listings. *Use case: Picking the next distribution experiment for a newly launched MVP, saving a draft launch post to the Distribution Kit, logging signups from last week's directory listing.*
- [Storied AI](https://www.storiedinc.com) **`C`** - Creates narratives, positioning, messaging, investor pitches, business cases, keynotes, sales narratives, strategy documents, website copy and about pages, reviews messages and prepares media interviews. *Use case: Drafting a board pre-read for a reorg, tightening a seed pitch narrative, preparing for a podcast interview.*
- [StoryChief](https://storychief.io) **`C`** - Searches, inspects, creates and updates articles and social posts, and creates webpages, newsletters, podcasts, video scripts, webinars and ebooks in a StoryChief workspace, returning editor links, comments and scheduling fields. *Use case: Drafting a blog article on spring product updates for team review, checking comments left on a LinkedIn post draft, creating a newsletter that rounds up last month's articles.*
- [SuperQuest](https://superquest.ai) **`C`** - Creates, updates and publishes interactive website experiences such as product choosers, calculators, surveys, quizzes and games, with themes, media and QR codes, and reports quest statistics, step funnels, answer statistics and page feedback. *Use case: Building a product-chooser quiz for a skincare line, generating a QR code for a trade-show survey, checking which quiz step loses the most visitors.*
- [Systeme.io](https://systeme.io) **`C`** - Finds information, creates content, and manages a systeme.io marketing workspace through natural-language requests. *Use case: Looking up what a workspace already contains, drafting a piece of content inside it, updating a record after a launch.*

- [The Content Atelier](https://emmeinfluence.com/atelier) **`C`** - Shows a week of draft posts from The Content Atelier on one approval board with voice and sells scores, records approve, edit or send-back decisions, and scores any caption, email or web line out of 100 with the three fixes to make first. *Use case: Signing off next week's five Instagram and LinkedIn posts in one pass, checking a launch email for signs of AI before it goes out.*

- [Timer.Coffee for Roasters](https://www.timer.coffee) **`C`** - Reads a Timer.Coffee roaster page overview and reviews, drafts and saves review replies and proposes profile updates, both for approval in the roaster dashboard, and creates, updates and deletes brewing recipes that stay private until published. *Use case: Drafting replies to new reviews of a roastery's Ethiopian beans, adding a V60 recipe.*
- [Tukey](https://tukey.ai) **`C`** - Profiles a connected YouTube channel and lists evidence-backed video opportunities with titles, premises, hooks and sources, and rates or starts an opportunity on request. *Use case: Picking the next video topic for a cooking channel, reviewing the sources behind a suggested idea, marking an idea as started.*
- [Unplain](https://unplain.io) **`C`** - Creates branded ebooks, reports and lead magnets from prompts, Markdown, web pages or PDFs, sets brand themes from a website, previews and exports PDFs, and lists leads and performance. *Use case: Turning a blog post into a 12-page branded guide, checking how many leads a checklist PDF collected.*
- [Vamos](https://getvamos.ai) **`C`** - Starts and polls Vamos chats that study a brand's niche and competitors on Instagram, TikTok and YouTube and write posts, scripts and ads, lists chats and messages, and checks credit usage. *Use case: Pulling the best opening hooks in a skincare niche this month, writing ten in the brand's voice.*

- [VybeOS](https://vybeos.co) **`C`** - Creative operating system that generates on-brand marketing campaigns and content and tracks their performance across brand and campaign workspaces. *Use case: Generate a batch of on-brand social ad creative for a new campaign and track how it performs.*
- [Walls.io Echo](https://echo.walls.io) **`C`** - Manages Echo pre-event marketing campaigns, their sharing visuals and folders, and reads campaign analytics over lifetime or date-ranged periods. *Use case: Reordering the sharing visuals on a conference campaign, updating share text before an event announcement, pulling the last 30 days of engagement.*

## CRM and Pipeline Management

- [100Bug](https://100bug.app) **`C`** - Reads deals, leads, notes and activities from the 100Bug real estate CRM, finding deals by client phone, email or name, and lists pipelines, stages, teams, channels, sources, tags, users, companies, loss reasons and team results. *Use case: Listing leads that came in today, finding a deal by the client's phone number, checking which stage a deal is in and who owns it.*
- [3DVOX](https://3dvox.com) **`C`** - Creates real-estate prospects with per-channel 3D tour links, writes WhatsApp, email or SMS messages from team templates, shows who opened links and what they viewed in the tour, updates stages and next contacts, logs contacts and finds development units by tower, status or price. *Use case: Finding which prospects opened their tour link since yesterday, writing a Spanish WhatsApp follow-up for a buyer who viewed the penthouse, logging an email sent to a broker.*
- [8ight Sales OS](https://8ight.ai) **`C`** - Finds and creates leads, contacts and deals, logs calls and notes, creates and completes tasks, moves deals through a five-stage pipeline, reports pipeline by stage, and enrolls leads in follow-up sequences, asking before changing existing records or enrolling a lead. *Use case: Adding a company met at a trade show with its contact and a note, moving a deal to Negotiation after a pricing call, listing overdue tasks on Monday morning.*
- [Adzviser HubSpot](https://adzviser.com/mcp/hubspot) **`C`** - Reads HubSpot reporting data through Adzviser, covering contacts, leads, deals and companies, and shows how the pipeline is moving over time. *Use case: Counting deals that moved to closed won this quarter, comparing new leads in September and October.*
- [Adzviser Pipedrive](https://adzviser.com/mcp/pipedrive) **`C`** - Reads Pipedrive reporting data through Adzviser, covering deals won, revenue and activities, and compares pipelines, stages and deal owners over time. *Use case: Comparing deals won by each sales rep this quarter, checking revenue by pipeline stage month over month.*
- [Adzviser Salesforce](https://adzviser.com/mcp/salesforce) **`C`** - Reads Salesforce CRM reporting data through Adzviser, covering leads, opportunities and pipeline, and compares owners, stages, win rates and deal velocity over time. *Use case: Checking win rates by opportunity owner for Q3, finding how long deals sit in the negotiation stage.*
- [Adzviser Zoho CRM](https://adzviser.com/mcp/zoho-crm) **`C`** - Reads Zoho CRM reporting data through Adzviser, covering leads, deals and contacts, and compares lead sources, pipeline stages and accounts over time. *Use case: Comparing deals created from trade shows and web forms, finding the pipeline stage holding the most deals.*

- [Aircover.ai](https://www.aircover.ai) **`C`** - Reads Aircover deals, meetings, transcripts, qualification results, agent results and reports, and saves and shares account documents and reports. *Use case: Reviewing what a buyer said about budget before a call, checking qualification gaps on a deal.*

- [Sofatofu](https://sofatofu.com) **`C`** - Finds, creates, updates and deletes CRM records such as contacts, logged calls and deals, moves deals between stages, lists and reverses recorded changes, and manages workspace members, invitations and settings. *Use case: Logging a call against a contact after a meeting, moving a deal to the negotiation stage, undoing a mistaken deal update.*
- [Solk](https://www.solk.app) **`C`** - Searches companies, contacts, opportunities and email threads in a B2B field-sales CRM, plans the day's meetings and actions, logs calls and visits, creates companies, contacts, opportunities, tasks, offers, orders and tickets, moves opportunities between stages, runs reports and reads or edits whiteboards, with every change to existing data held for approval. *Use case: Preparing for a client visit with the company card and deal history, logging a call with its next action, moving an opportunity to the next stage.*
- [SonicR1](https://sonicr1.com) **`C`** - Reads SonicR1 CRM records, call summaries and the sales knowledge base, prepares call briefings and follow-up drafts, proposes CRM and knowledge base changes for approval, records outcomes and enrols contacts in sequences. *Use case: Prepping for a renewal call with last quarter's notes, proposing a deal update after a demo, drafting a follow-up email.*
- [Standards](https://standards.new) **`C`** - Searches, counts and totals records in a combined CRM, drive, email and meetings system of record by status, owner or month, creates, updates, organizes and deletes records, reads attached documents and files new ones according to the workspace schema. *Use case: Totaling open deals by owner for this quarter, updating a contact after a call, filing a signed contract in the right drive folder.*
- [Stone Project](https://www.stoneproject.app) **`C`** - Searches and reads countertop fabrication projects, clients, quotes, invoices, payments, orders, suppliers and slab inventory, and creates or updates projects, clients, suppliers, colors and inventory pieces with images. *Use case: Checking open quotes for a kitchen remodel client, adding a new quartz slab to inventory with photos, finding remnant pieces large enough for a bathroom vanity.*
- [Stratyfix](https://stratyfix.com) **`C`** - Reads Stratyfix sales data, including deals, deal briefs and risk, pace, team coverage, target odds and coaching queues, with reps seeing their own book and managers their team under the app's permissions, and says so when Stratyfix withholds a number, with no changes possible from chat. *Use case: Asking whether the team will make the quarter, finding which deals need attention this week, reading a brief on one deal.*

- [SUNNNY](https://sunnny.com) **`C`** - Reads SUNNNY unified customer data from CRM, support and call systems, including organizations, contacts, records, summaries and connector status. *Use case: Getting a full picture of an account across Salesforce, Zendesk and Gong before a renewal call.*

- [TMA — Time, Money, Assets](https://app.timemoneyassets.com) **`C`** - Returns a daily brief, searches a relationship Book and contact stories, adds or imports prospects, books, reschedules, completes or cancels meetings with reminders, resolves follow-ups, logs activity, field notes and to-dos, saves approved Studio post ideas and updates or deletes them, reads product help, and creates and reads support tickets. *Use case: Starting the morning with the daily brief and first move, logging three client calls made after lunch, rescheduling Thursday's review meeting to next week.*

- [Translead CRM](https://translead.jp) **`C`** - Searches, creates, and updates CRM accounts, contacts, deals, and activities, exports records to CSV, and reads saved aggregation and goal reports. *Use case: Logging a completed call on a deal with a meeting link, summarizing open deals for one account, exporting this quarter's expected closes as CSV.*
- [Truehome Desk](https://truehome.ie) **`C`** - Manages an Irish estate agent's listings, buyer enquiries, viewings, and offers, with confirmation required before any message, publish, or status change. *Use case: Reviewing enquiries that came in overnight, checking a property against its reserve and the seller's goal, recording an offer and replying to the buyer.*
- [TruLata](https://trulata.com) **`C`** - Lists and finds leads by email or phone, adds new leads, sets lead status to contacted, quoted, won or lost, and lists recent blog posts in a service-business marketing account. *Use case: Reviewing who contacted the business this week, marking a lead as quoted, adding a lead taken over the phone.*
- [Trussi AI](https://trussi.ai) - Roofing CRM covering projects, customers, pipeline, schedules, and dashboards. *Use case: Managing a roofing sales pipeline, scheduling crews, tracking customer projects.*

- [Vendasta](https://www.vendasta.com) **`C`** - Searches and reads CRM contacts, companies, opportunities, associations and pipelines, creates or updates contacts, companies and opportunities, links records, creates pipelines, and searches published Vendasta documentation. *Use case: Reviewing a company's open deals before a call, creating a contact and opportunity from meeting notes, correcting deal fields after a pipeline review.*

## Email SMS and Lifecycle Marketing

- [A/B Simulate](https://absimulate.jacquard.com) **`C`** - Ranks 2 to 20 variants of email subject lines, preheaders, SMS, push notifications and ad, web or social headlines with Jacquard's copy performance model, predicting the winner and its lift over a control, and records actual campaign results. *Use case: Picking the strongest of five Black Friday subject lines before a send, sharing the real click results of last week's push test.*
- [ActiveCampaign](https://www.activecampaign.com) - Generates, edits, schedules and sends ActiveCampaign email campaigns, runs campaign performance reports, manages contacts, tags, lists and custom fields, and builds automations and manages the sales pipeline. *Use case: Drafting and scheduling an email campaign, running a performance report on past campaigns, adding a tag to a set of contacts.*
- [Adflipr](https://adflipr.com) **`C`** - Searches email campaigns, automations, popups and landing pages, reads performance and cart-recovery summaries, creates campaigns, templates, popups and landing pages, manages sending-domain setup, schedules or sends campaigns, publishes popups and pauses or activates automations. *Use case: Reviewing abandoned-cart recovery for last month, drafting a holiday campaign from a template, scheduling it for Friday morning.*
- [Adobe Journey Optimizer](https://business.adobe.com/products/journey-optimizer.html) - Reads Adobe Journey Optimizer sandboxes, journeys, campaigns, channel configurations and loyalty challenges to check statuses, surface stopped or orphaned drafts and spot channel configuration issues, through a server in beta. *Use case: Checking which journeys or campaigns have stopped, finding orphaned drafts, spotting a channel configuration issue.*
- [Adzviser ActiveCampaign](https://adzviser.com/mcp/activecampaign) **`C`** - Reads ActiveCampaign email reporting data through Adzviser, covering sends, opens, clicks, contact growth and engagement, and compares campaigns and lists over time. *Use case: Comparing open rates of the last four newsletters, checking contact growth on a webinar list since September.*
- [Adzviser Klaviyo](https://adzviser.com/mcp/klaviyo) **`C`** - Reads Klaviyo reporting data through Adzviser, covering campaigns, flows and revenue, and compares open, click and conversion rates, list growth and segment performance over time. *Use case: Comparing revenue from the welcome and abandoned cart flows in Q3, checking open rates for a Black Friday campaign.*
- [Adzviser Mailchimp](https://adzviser.com/mcp/mailchimp) **`C`** - Reads Mailchimp reporting data through Adzviser, covering opens, clicks and unsubscribes, and compares campaigns, audiences, subject lines and list growth over time. *Use case: Checking which subject lines drove the best open rates this quarter, comparing unsubscribes across two audiences.*
- [Adzviser Marketo](https://adzviser.com/mcp/marketo) **`C`** - Reads Marketo reporting data through Adzviser, covering leads created and emails sent, and compares programs and campaign performance over time. *Use case: Comparing leads created by two webinar programs, checking email sends for a nurture stream last month.*
- [Adzviser Omnisend](https://adzviser.com/mcp/omnisend) **`C`** - Reads Omnisend reporting data through Adzviser, covering emails sent, open rate and click rate, and compares campaigns and automation performance over time. *Use case: Comparing click rates of the last three promotional emails, checking how the cart recovery automation performed in November.*
- [Alia](https://www.aliapopups.com) **`C`** - Analyzes the performance of popups that collect email and SMS signups. *Use case: Comparing signup rates across popups, checking how many SMS subscribers a popup brought in, spotting a popup that stopped converting.*
- [Attentive](https://www.attentive.com) - Creates, schedules, pauses and resumes SMS, RCS, push and email campaigns, builds and publishes automated journeys, creates and sizes audience segments, edits email templates and brand styles, generates on-brand copy and short links, and queries campaign and journey metrics, asking for confirmation before creating or changing content, scheduling sends or changing live programs. *Use case: Building an automated SMS journey from a template, sizing an audience segment before a sale, pulling performance metrics for last week's campaigns.*
- [AWeber](https://www.aweber.com) **`C`** - Write newsletters, add and update subscribers, search email lists, and pull engagement performance stats, all without leaving the conversation. *Use case: Drafting a newsletter for a product launch, adding a batch of subscribers from a recent webinar, checking the open rate on last week's campaign before sending a follow-up.*
- [BayEngage](https://targetbay.com) **`C`** - Builds email campaigns, templates and audience segments from store customer data, schedules, pauses or cancels campaigns, sends test emails, and reports campaign and automation performance. *Use case: Building a segment of repeat buyers who have not ordered in 90 days, scheduling a weekend sale email, comparing performance across last month's campaigns.*
- [beehiiv](https://www.beehiiv.com) - Connects a beehiiv workspace covering posts, podcasts, automations, segments, products, and monetization data for drafting, design, and analysis. *Use case: Drafting a newsletter issue, checking which segment opens most, reviewing subscription revenue for the quarter.*
- [BlueConic](https://www.blueconic.com) - A customer data platform that unifies customer profiles and signals for real-time marketing activation. *Use case: Querying tenant customer-profile data to understand which segments are driving repeat purchases.*
- [Bouncer Email Verification MCP Server](https://www.usebouncer.com) **`C`** - Verifies single email addresses or batch lists with deliverability status and quality flags such as role-based or catch-all, checks domains, runs toxicity checks for breached or spam-trap addresses, and reports remaining credits. *Use case: Cleaning a 3,000-address list before Friday's newsletter send, checking why a prospect's email keeps bouncing, flagging role-based addresses in a trade-show lead list.*
- [Braze MCP Server](https://www.braze.com) - Reads Braze data across 16 categories, including campaigns, canvases, catalogs, segments, events and KPIs, with limited writes that create and update content blocks and email templates and upload media library assets. *Use case: Analyzing a campaign's send and revenue data series, reviewing a canvas data summary, creating an email template.*
- [Brevo](https://www.brevo.com) - Analyzes Brevo email campaign performance and A/B test results, looks up contacts, lists and segments, drafts email and SMS campaigns and HTML templates for review in Brevo and sends template tests, but never sends or schedules email campaigns or deletes anything. *Use case: Comparing A/B test results on a recent email campaign, drafting an SMS campaign for a segment, looking up a contact's stats.*
- [Campaign Monitor](https://www.campaignmonitor.com) - Searches sent Campaign Monitor campaigns, reads their content and performance such as opens, clicks, bounces and unsubscribes, audits campaigns, creates segments, and drafts new campaigns block by block with branding, leaving each draft in the account for a person to review and send. *Use case: Summarizing the last four newsletters and what drove clicks, building a segment of subscribers who opened in the past 90 days, drafting a Black Friday email.*
- [CampaignHQ](https://www.campaignhq.co) **`C`** - Reports email, WhatsApp and SMS campaign, automation and chat-flow results, diagnoses why contacts miss messages, drafts and tests campaigns and templates, builds segments, draft automations and chat flows, adds, updates, tags, unsubscribes and deletes contacts, queries events and funnels, and searches, assigns, closes and, when an admin allows it, replies to inbox conversations, asking first before changing or deleting existing data. *Use case: Comparing last month's WhatsApp and email campaign revenue, diagnosing why a contact stopped getting SMS, drafting a win-back email for a lapsed segment.*
- [Carillon](https://carillon.dev) **`C`** - Configures app push credentials and API keys, debugs devices and tags, manages audiences, traces messages, and sends one-off or targeted push notification campaigns, including test sends. *Use case: Uploading push credentials for a new app, sending a test notification to one device, targeting a re-engagement push at a tagged user audience.*
- [channel](https://askcuri.com/en/docs/claude) **`C`** - Reads topics, email campaigns, subscriber stats, posts, trend reports and creators in Curi’s channel workspace, and creates or edits drafts of topics, campaigns and posts without sending. *Use case: Drafting a newsletter campaign around a new trend report, checking subscriber counts before planning a launch email, outlining next month's topics.*
- [Chargezen SmartyTap](https://chargezen.com/smartytap) **`C`** - Runs email, SMS and WhatsApp marketing in Chargezen SmartyTap, building campaigns, automated flows, segments and surveys, reporting campaign and flow performance, and working the customer inbox, with new campaigns and flows starting as drafts and deleting, launching or publishing held for approval. *Use case: Checking how much revenue the abandoned checkout flow recovered last month, building a segment of repeat buyers for a holiday SMS.*
- [Churn Solution](https://churnsolution.com) **`C`** - Reads Churn Solution cancel flow metrics, outcomes, save rates and revenue by flow and offer, cancellation reasons by plan and feedback categories and sentiment trends, and searches cancel flow sessions to see why customers leave. *Use case: Finding the top cancellation reasons for a plan, comparing how retention offers perform, tracking feedback sentiment over time.*
- [CleverReach](https://www.cleverreach.com) **`C`** - Lists and creates recipient groups, attributes and segment filters on a German, EU-hosted email marketing platform, creates and edits mailing drafts from templates, reports on campaign opens, clicks, bounces, email clients and quality metrics and searches the help center, without sending mailings. *Use case: Summarizing the performance of the last five newsletters, building a segment of recipients in Germany who opened in the last 90 days, drafting a product announcement from the spring template.*
- [ClickTimer](https://www.clicktimer.co) **`C`** - Creates, updates, deletes and lists email countdown timers, returns embed code, reports impressions and credits, and with a connected Klaviyo account lists templates, publishes timers as content blocks and sends test emails. *Use case: Building a countdown for a Black Friday sale email, changing a timer's end date and colors, publishing a timer to Klaviyo for an abandoned-cart flow.*
- [Commune](https://usecommune.com) **`C`** - Reports newsletter article performance, subscriber sources and reader engagement, reads community conversations and highlights, and drafts, revises, test-sends, schedules and sends articles to subscribers. *Use case: Checking how last Tuesday's article did against the newsletter overall, finding readers who are drifting away, sending a test copy before scheduling the next issue.*
- [Constant Contact](https://www.constantcontact.com) - Generates Constant Contact email campaigns from a description, renders a live HTML preview, and applies edits until the campaign is ready to send. *Use case: Drafting a spring sale email, previewing how the layout renders before sending, tightening the subject line after reading it back.*
- [Conversion](https://conversion.ai) **`C`** - Builds email campaigns, segments audiences, configures workflows, and pulls live campaign performance from the Conversion marketing platform. *Use case: Segmenting an audience for a re-engagement send, setting up a workflow triggered by a signup, checking open and click performance after launch.*
- [Cordial](https://cordial.com) **`C`** - Reads a Cordial account, describing audiences, contact schema, journeys, campaigns, message performance and insight reports, and exports message stats. *Use case: Explaining why last Tuesday's promo email underperformed, estimating the size of a lapsed-buyer audience.*
- [Cueston](https://cueston.com) **`C`** - Creates, edits and previews in-product tours, tooltips, banners, checklists, surveys and announcements for SaaS onboarding and publishes them on approval, targets audience segments, manages translations, reviews and versions, and analyzes completion funnels and survey results. *Use case: Building a five-step onboarding tour for new admins, finding tooltips whose targets broke after a UI change, reviewing survey responses from trial users.*
- [Customer.io](https://customer.io) - Builds and edits campaigns, broadcasts and newsletters, creates segments from plain-English definitions, updates customer profiles, drafts and publishes Design Studio emails, analyzes deliveries, opens, clicks and conversions, and manages Data Pipelines, with dry-run previews available for writes and deletes. *Use case: Building a segment of users inactive for 90 days, drafting a re-engagement email and running QA on it, finding out why deliveries dropped this week.*
- [DataFlows SMS](https://dataflows.com.au) **`C`** - Sends and schedules business SMS to individual numbers or contact groups, previews recipients and credit costs before sending, and tracks delivery status, replies, campaign results and usage. *Use case: Previewing the credit cost of a sale announcement to a customer group, scheduling an appointment reminder for Friday morning, reading replies to yesterday's campaign.*
- [Delivra](https://www.delivra.com) **`C`** - Explores Delivra email and SMS campaign performance, subscriber activity, engagement metrics, top domains, and poll results. *Use case: Comparing open rates across two recent sends, checking which domains bounce most, reading how subscribers answered a poll in a newsletter.*
