- [TrueLoyal](https://www.trueloyal.com) **`C`** - Reads and aggregates TrueLoyal loyalty program members, points transactions, rewards, coupons, activities, tiers and rules, and creates and updates rewards, coupons, activities, rules and tiers. *Use case: Counting members who earned points last month, listing rewards redeemed most in Q3.*
- [UE Advocacy](https://userevidence.com) **`C`** - Works with a UserEvidence customer advocacy program: finds reference customers by industry, size or use case, shows which open opportunities have advocate support, tracks advocate engagement, reference requests and how often each customer has been asked, browses the rewards catalog, and lists campaign missions and participants. *Use case: Finding a reference customer of similar size for a late-stage deal, checking how often an advocate has been asked this quarter, listing stalled reference requests and their owners.*
- [Unifyr](https://www.unifyr.com) **`C`** - The agentic Partner Relationship Management (PRM) and Channel Sales and Marketing (TCMA) platform for indirect channel sales. *Use case: Checking which channel partner is closing the most indirect deals this quarter, coordinating a joint marketing campaign with a reseller.*
- [VEXiON cards](https://www.vexioncards.one) **`C`** - Reads VEXiON wallet loyalty cards, customers, locations and transaction reports for business admins with API access, and with write access creates cards, customers and transactions and sends or cancels push notifications, with messages to people previewed and sent only after confirmation. *Use case: Looking up a cafe customer's stamp count by phone number, sending a double-stamp Monday push to all cardholders.*
- [Vuelvy](https://vuelvy.com) **`C`** - Reads business profiles, Apple Wallet and Google Wallet programs, audiences, automations, benefits and campaigns, plus email campaigns, flows, segments and templates, previews wallet campaigns and creates wallet programs or saves drafts after an exact preview and explicit approval. *Use case: Reviewing which Apple Wallet campaigns ran for a coffee shop last month, previewing a Google Wallet campaign before approval, drafting a new wallet program for a second location.*
- [Wallet Pass Builder](https://walletpassbuilder.com) **`C`** - Creates, updates and archives Apple Wallet and Google Wallet passes, runs loyalty stamp cards, records loyalty visits, lists customers and sends or cancels lock-screen notifications. *Use case: Creating a membership card for a gym, adding a stamp for a coffee customer, sending a notification about a weekend offer.*
- [Walletap](https://www.walletap.io) **`C`** - Design a wallet pass template, issue passes against it, and push updates to phones that already hold one - loyalty cards, tickets and memberships across Apple and Google Wallet. *Use case: Designing a template for a new loyalty card, issuing tickets for an event, pushing an update to everyone who already has one on their phone.*
- [YourPartnerOS](https://www.yourpartneros.com) **`C`** - Explains ISV partner program rules, returns weekly partner news, runs a practice maturity check, and with an account shows partner health, deal registration status, account overlaps, meeting prep and draft notes or messages held for review. *Use case: Checking deal registration status before a call, prepping for a partner QBR, finding which partners share a target account.*
- [Zeydoo](https://zeydoo.com) **`C`** - Reads Zeydoo CPA network offers, payout rates, account balance, and traffic statistics for affiliates and publishers. *Use case: Checking last week's earnings against the balance, breaking conversions down by country, finding catalogue offers that pay for Android traffic from Brazil.*
- [애드픽 (ADPICK)](https://adpick.co.kr) **`C`** - Creates ADPICK affiliate shopping links from product URLs, reads the member's own performance, sales and link activity, and searches partner mall products, recommended products, ad campaigns and service guides. *Use case: Turning a kitchen gadget product URL into a revenue link for a blog review, checking last month's sales by product before planning next week's posts.*

## Marketing Analytics and Consumer Insights

- [ABConvert](https://www.abconvert.io) **`C`** - Inspects and compares Shopify A/B test results, creates draft tests, previews storefront changes, and manages experiment lifecycles. *Use case: Checking whether a price test has a winner, drafting a checkout experiment, previewing a storefront variant before launch.*
- [Acoustic](https://www.acoustic.com) **`C`** - The performance edge for your marketing data - right in Claude. *Use case: Checking which marketing campaign performed best last month, spotting a dip in performance before it becomes a trend.*
- [AdHatch](https://adhatch.io) **`C`** - Reads saved advertising and website report snapshots for an AdHatch business, finds available dates, compares periods, and opens saved insights with the evidence behind each one. *Use case: Comparing saved advertising and website report figures between September and October, checking the baseline behind an insight about falling clicks.*
- [Admetrics](https://www.admetrics.io) **`C`** - Exposes Admetrics Query Language analytics as validated tools that agents and coding assistants can call for marketing measurement. *Use case: Querying campaign performance for a date range, checking a metric definition before reporting it, pulling channel results into an analysis.*
- [Adobe CX Enterprise Coworker](https://business.adobe.com/products/cx-enterprise-coworker/ai-assistant.html) - Answers natural-language questions about customer experience insights, analyzes customer experiences and completes marketing tasks across Adobe CX Enterprise, with English-only support. *Use case: Asking which customer segments drove the most conversions last quarter, analyzing where customers drop off in an onboarding journey.*
- [Adobe Marketing Agent](https://business.adobe.com/blog/introducing-adobe-marketing-agent-microsoft-365-copilot) - Marketing campaign and audience insights from the Adobe stack. *Use case: Campaign performance analysis, audience segmentation, cross-product Adobe marketing diagnostics.*
- [AdRadar](https://adradar.digital) **`C`** - Reads tracked competitors' ads from the Meta, Google, LinkedIn and TikTok ad libraries, their landing pages and prices, customer reviews, website changes and brand keyword bidding, and compares competitors in AdRadar projects. *Use case: Checking which hooks a rival's newest Meta ads use, seeing who bid on a brand name in Google Ads this month, summarizing complaints about a competitor on G2.*
- [AdSpends AdLibrary — Meta Ads, Brands & Landingpages](https://adspends.com) **`C`** - Searches a database of Meta ads delivered in the EU, plus advertisers and landing pages, ranked by estimated spend and reach, opens brand overviews, and reads saved boards, collections and tracked brands. *Use case: Finding the highest-spending German supplement advertorials this month, pulling a skincare brand's top ads by reach.*
- [AdSpendSpy for Meta Ad Library](https://www.adspendspy.com) **`C`** - Searches Meta Ad Library advertisers and their ads, compares and finds similar advertisers, ranks top ads and landing pages by estimated spend, tracks brands and reads saved lists, projects and spend alerts in AdSpendSpy. *Use case: Ranking a fashion competitor's ads by estimated spend in euros, adding a new DTC brand to tracking, checking which landing pages a rival pushes hardest.*
- [Adzviser](https://adzviser.com) **`C`** - Retrieves reporting data from ad, analytics, social, ecommerce, CRM and email platforms such as Google Ads, Meta Ads, GA4, Shopify, HubSpot and Salesforce to compare channel performance, track sales and review pipeline, and previews changes to supported campaigns. *Use case: Tracking this quarter's sales and pipeline, checking why marketing spend spiked last week.*
- [Adzviser Business Profile](https://adzviser.com/mcp/google-business-profile) **`C`** - Reads Google Business Profile reporting data through Adzviser, covering views, phone calls and bookings, and compares locations and how customers find and contact each listing over time. *Use case: Comparing phone calls from three clinic locations last month, checking whether a café's profile views dropped after the holidays.*
- [Adzviser CallRail](https://adzviser.com/mcp/callrail) **`C`** - Reads CallRail reporting data through Adzviser, covering calls, sources and lead quality, and compares tracking numbers, campaigns, first-time callers and call duration over time. *Use case: Finding which ad campaign drove the most first-time callers last month, comparing average call length across two tracking numbers.*
- [Adzviser CallTrackingMetrics](https://adzviser.com/mcp/call-tracking-metrics) **`C`** - Reads CallTrackingMetrics reporting data through Adzviser, covering calls, texts and lead quality, and compares tracking sources, campaigns, call outcomes and agent performance over time. *Use case: Comparing answered calls by agent for last week, finding the tracking source with the most qualified leads in October.*
- [Adzviser Meta Ad Library](https://adzviser.com/mcp/meta-ad-library) **`C`** - Searches the public Meta Ad Library through Adzviser to see which ads a brand runs on Facebook and Instagram, compare messaging and creative angles across competitors and narrow results by country. *Use case: Reviewing a rival sneaker brand's active ads in France, comparing the offers three meal-kit companies are promoting.*
- [AgencyAnalytics](https://agencyanalytics.com) **`C`** - Reports on client marketing performance across SEO, PPC, social, email, call tracking and ecommerce channels connected through AgencyAnalytics' 85+ integrations. *Use case: Pulling a month-end summary for one client's paid and organic channels, comparing two accounts' call volumes, checking which campaigns moved since the last report.*
- [AIlon](https://ailon.io/ailon-api-zielgruppen-insights-media-aktivierung) **`C`** - Generates audience definitions from natural-language descriptions and returns aggregated demographic and behavioral insights, comparing segments for German media planning and targeting. *Use case: Defining the audience for a premium product launch, comparing two campaign segments on media consumption, checking which attributes separate buyers from non-buyers.*
- [AI事業変革シミュレーター](https://feelflow.net) **`C`** - Reframes a business by audience, offering, and delivery, returning Japanese-language business ideas with estimated success probabilities, SWOT, personas, and action plans. *Use case: Running an analysis on a new service line, opening the customer journey behind a promising idea, revisiting an analysis run earlier on the web.*
- [All Ears](https://allears.ai) **`C`** - Creates and updates All Ears monitors that collect brand and keyword mentions from podcasts, radio, TV, YouTube and TikTok, and reads mentions, sentiment, reach, peaks, top sources and time series. *Use case: Setting up a monitor for a competitor's brand name in Swedish podcasts, finding the peak day of mentions after a product launch, listing the top sources by reach.*
- [Analytics Brain](https://brain.witbee.com/en/mcp) **`C`** - Searches and reads Witbee's library of marketing analytics, tracking and data engineering guides, webinar notes and newsletters, with citations. *Use case: Finding Witbee's guide on server-side tracking, citing its setup steps in an answer.*
- [api.mergn.com](https://www.mergn.com) **`C`** - Returns read-only performance reporting for MERGN email, SMS, push and WhatsApp campaigns and journeys, including sends, deliveries, views, clicks, conversions and revenue by date range and channel, along with the platform's own metric definitions. *Use case: Checking which channel drove the most revenue last quarter, comparing March campaign clicks against February, confirming how a conversion is defined.*
- [app.reportgarden.com](https://reportgarden.com) **`C`** - Queries agency clients' marketing data from advertising, analytics, SEO and ecommerce integrations such as Google Ads, Meta, Google Analytics 4 and Shopify, looks up clients, accounts, campaigns and budgets, and creates, updates, schedules, shares and emails reports. *Use case: Comparing month-over-month spend and ROAS for a client's Google and Meta campaigns, creating a monthly report from a template, scheduling it to email the client.*
- [Appfigures](https://appfigures.com) **`C`** - Searches apps with download and revenue estimates, demographics and SDKs, tracks ASO keyword ranks and suggestions, reads Apple Ads campaigns, keywords and reports, queries connected app metrics, and lists and replies to reviews. *Use case: Finding competitors' top organic keywords, tracking keyword rank trends for an app, replying to recent one-star reviews.*
- [AppGoat](https://appgoat.ai) **`C`** - Looks up App Store and Google Play apps with metadata, ratings, AI-tagged reviews, rating and install history, listing changes, charts and competitors, researches and tracks keywords and rankings, runs app audits with metadata rewrites, watches apps for changes, and proposes review replies, listing updates and Apple Ads bid changes that apply only after approval. *Use case: Auditing an app's listing and getting a rewritten subtitle within character limits, pulling crash-related reviews from the last month, comparing keyword rankings against two competitors.*
- [Appier Enterprise MCP Server](https://www.appier.com/en) **`C`** - Provides Appier Enterprise customer insights, campaign and journey performance, audiences, and marketing content for teams using AIQUA and AIRIS. *Use case: Reviewing how a cross-channel journey performed, checking which audience segment converted best, pulling campaign content for a planning review.*
- [AppmanAI](https://appmanai.com) **`C`** - Returns App Store and Google Play data for app store optimization, including app metadata, ratings, category rankings, keyword popularity, difficulty and volatility, keyword rankings, competitor overlap and reviews, and drafts keyword lists and metadata. *Use case: Finding high-popularity, low-difficulty keywords for a fitness app, checking ranking history for a target keyword, drafting a new subtitle from store data.*
- [AppRate](https://apprate.io) **`C`** - Reads App Store and Google Play reviews, rating history and keyword ranks for tracked apps, suggests keywords, tracks and untracks keywords, and replies to reviews after confirmation, on the AppRate Pro plan. *Use case: Finding what users complain about since version 4.2, tracking five new keywords, replying to this week's 1-star reviews.*
- [AppsFlyer](https://www.appsflyer.com) **`C`** - Queries AppsFlyer campaign performance, ROAS, retention, creative performance, SKAN, incrementality experiments and Protect360 fraud data, audits and creates OneLink links, and reads audiences and app settings. *Use case: Comparing ROAS by media source for an iOS game last week, checking Protect360 fraud on a new ad network, creating a OneLink for a QR campaign.*
- [Appstack](https://www.appstack.tech) **`C`** - Query your app's attribution, revenue, and campaign analytics — and manage tracking links — directly from Claude. *Use case: Checking which campaign drove the most installs this week, creating a new tracking link before a launch, seeing how much revenue a specific channel generated.*
- [AppTrove](https://apptrove.com) **`C`** - Provides read-only access to AppTrove mobile-measurement data: installs, revenue, partner and event breakdowns, retention, UniLink performance, and fraud signals. *Use case: Checking yesterday's installs and revenue by app, comparing partner performance after a campaign shift, reviewing retention for last month's cohort.*
- [Artificial Societies](https://societies.ai) **`C`** - Tests messages and decisions on simulated audiences of AI personas by running surveys and focus groups, interviewing individual personas and analyzing the responses. *Use case: Running a survey on two product taglines with a simulated audience, interviewing a skeptical persona about a pricing change.*
- [Ask Your Audience](https://www.askyouraudience.ai) **`C`** - Runs AYA snapshots, concept tests, focus groups, interviews, brand evaluations and image ad tests with digital twins built from real people, shows token cost first, and reads results and findings. *Use case: Testing two taglines on a 30-something audience, running a focus group on a new price point after approving the token cost.*
- [AskDeeper](https://askdeeper.ai) **`C`** - Creates and edits AskDeeper customer research studies and questions, publishes interviews after approval, reads findings and research briefs, and exports interview transcripts. *Use case: Setting up a churn interview study with five questions, publishing it after review, exporting transcripts from last month's study.*
- [aso.best](https://aso.best) **`C`** - Researches App Store and Google Play keywords from store data, reads competitors' ranked phrases with popularity and difficulty, tracks keywords and alerts, translates and updates store listings after approval, and builds client reports. *Use case: Finding phrases a rival planner app ranks for outside its title, translating a subtitle into five languages, tracking 20 keywords in the US store.*
- [ASOClimb](https://asoclimb.com) **`C`** - Inspects App Store and Google Play keywords with traffic, difficulty and KEI scores, finds keyword gaps and opportunities against competitors, tracks rank history and category ranks, and checks listing metadata drafts. *Use case: Finding keyword gaps against a competing language app in Germany, checking rank history for a fitness keyword since the last update.*
- [ASOMap](https://asomap.com) **`C`** - Tracks app keyword rankings, ratings, reviews and competitors in the App Store, Google Play and RuStore for ASOMap Pro plan accounts, suggests keywords, scores listings, saves listing and changelog drafts and builds screenshot sets, asking before adding or removing keywords and competitors, starting a collection or saving drafts. *Use case: Checking which keywords a calorie counter gained this week, tracking a new competitor on RuStore, drafting a localized changelog.*
- [ASOMobile](https://asomobile.net) **`C`** - Returns App Store and Google Play keyword traffic and competition, keyword suggestions, app rankings and history, competitors, top charts, reviews, organic download estimates and Apple Search Ads keywords on paid plans, and adds or removes tracked apps and keywords with confirmation. *Use case: Checking a keyword's traffic in 10 countries at once, estimating organic downloads for a rival app, adding 30 keywords to track.*
- [Athena by Zeta](https://zetaglobal.com) **`C`** - Analyzes audience reach, customer demographics, psychographics, transactions and content consumption, campaign performance and marketing datasets in an authorized Zeta Marketing Platform account, returning source-backed answers and executive briefings. *Use case: Profiling the demographics of a loyalty audience segment, checking which campaigns drove the most reach last quarter, turning dataset metrics into a short briefing for leadership.*
- [Attache](https://attache.studio) **`C`** - Queries Google Analytics 4, Google Ads, and Search Console data read-only across multiple client accounts, including cross-client reports and GAQL queries. *Use case: Comparing cost per acquisition across every client this month, finding which Search Console queries gained clicks, reporting sessions and revenue by channel.*
- [Attest](https://www.askattest.com) **`C`** - Searches Attest consumer research studies and returns their audiences, questions and survey results filtered by demographics or saved segments, compares results across waves and markets, and reads boards and generated key findings, with respondents across 59 markets. *Use case: Pulling brand awareness results for women aged 18 to 34 from the latest wave, comparing purchase intent between the UK and US markets, reading the key findings on a campaign board.*

- [Audience Reaction Engine (by Zinnawa)](https://zinnawa.com/product) **`C`** - Tests launches, price changes, campaign messages and posts against consumer or B2B AI persona panels, returning a go or no-go verdict, objections, risks and rewrites, compares two versions, and interviews single personas. *Use case: Testing a price increase announcement on the consumer panel, comparing two versions of a crisis statement, interviewing a CFO persona about a B2B pitch.*
- [Audienzes Pro](https://audienzes.com/mcp/premium) **`C`** - Profiles audience segments by interests, themes and platforms with Audienzes Pro, compares audiences and positioning, maps trends, search intent and listening, scopes markets and reads campaign performance. *Use case: Finding which interests over-index for women 18 to 24 in Denmark, comparing two audience definitions before a campaign.*
- [august](https://getaugust.ai) - Interviews Digital Twins — models of real customers trained on a business's own data — and runs focus groups with them. *Use case: Testing messaging on a customer segment before launch, running a focus group on two pricing options, probing why a persona rejected a feature.*
- [AWSYS Links](https://awsys.co) **`C`** - Creates AWSYS short links with custom slugs, QR codes and UTM templates, organizes links into folders and tags, manages domains and webhooks on paid plans, and reads click stats by country, referrer and device. *Use case: Creating a short link with a custom slug for a podcast episode, generating its QR code, checking this week's clicks by country.*
- [AxiTrace](https://axitrace.com) **`C`** - Reads AxiTrace server-side tracking data for Shopify, WooCommerce, Shopware 6 and Magento 2 stores on a paid plan, including revenue, ROAS, campaigns, channels, traffic sources, conversion paths and order journeys, and, after asking first, manages leads, surveys and custom events. *Use case: Comparing ROAS for Google and Meta campaigns last month, tracing the journey behind a large order, creating a post-purchase survey.*
- [bananabread AI](https://bananabread.ai) **`C`** - Builds consumer sentiment reports for a product from reviews and posts on Amazon, Walmart, Reddit, TikTok, YouTube, Instagram and the wider web, sorting what shoppers love, wish for and complain about with quotes and source links, and looks up submitted products and credit balance. *Use case: Finding what shoppers dislike about a breakfast cereal, listing features customers wish a lip balm had, checking remaining credit before submitting a new product.*
- [Blinkfire](https://www.blinkfire.com) **`C`** - Retrieves sports sponsorship reports and brand media value, social post engagement, audience demographics, broadcast, in-stadium and scene value reports and global rankings for teams, leagues, athletes, brands and venues, and manages playlists, entity groups, inventory, proposals and deals, with updates and deletions asking for confirmation. *Use case: Measuring how much media value a jersey sponsor earned from a club's social posts this season, benchmarking two teams' follower growth, drafting a sponsorship proposal from in-stadium inventory.*
- [Bluefish](https://bluefishai.com) - Reads Bluefish data on how AI assistants describe a brand, including visibility, favorability, accuracy and brand safety scores, citations, sources and concepts, and why they changed. *Use case: Finding which sources drove a drop in brand favorability in AI answers, comparing positioning against a competitor.*
- [Brand24](https://brand24.com) - Queries Brand24 social listening projects for mentions, sentiment, reach, share of voice, and the events behind spikes in volume. *Use case: Summarizing a project's last 30 days, explaining a sudden spike in mentions, comparing sentiment against two competitors.*
- [BrandIndex](https://business.yougov.com/product/brandindex) **`C`** - Queries YouGov BrandIndex brand health metrics such as awareness, buzz, quality and value by region, sector and demographic, and saves analysis charts. *Use case: Comparing buzz for three UK supermarket brands over the last quarter among 18 to 34 year olds.*
- [Buzzabout](https://buzzabout.ai) **`C`** - Collects public posts and comments from TikTok, Instagram, YouTube, Reddit, X, LinkedIn and Facebook on a topic or audience, detects recurring themes, and answers questions with the numbers and posts behind each finding, with saved collections and tracking agents. *Use case: Finding what Reddit and TikTok users complain about in a rival oat milk brand, tracking mentions of a product launch over its first two weeks.*
- [BuzzSearch](https://buzzsearch.ai) **`C`** - Searches Reddit, TikTok, YouTube and Facebook comments for quoted, linked pain points and brand or competitor mentions, reads comments on a given thread or video, and generates ad hooks from buyers' own words. *Use case: Collecting complaints about robot vacuums for ad copy, reading reactions under a competitor's launch video, gathering phrases new store owners use to describe their problems.*
- [Cafeteria](https://www.teamcafeteria.com) - Queries Cafeteria Gen Next consumer research tables, counts brand mentions, lists top entities and requests custom tables. *Use case: Counting how often young respondents mention a sneaker brand, finding top snack brands among 18 to 24 year olds.*
- [CaliberMind](https://calibermind.com) **`C`** - Queries modeled go-to-market data, including pipelines, CRM records, account journeys, and attribution, with schema context loaded automatically. *Use case: Asking which campaigns sourced this quarter's pipeline, tracing an account's journey to closed-won, building an attribution dashboard.*
- [CallRail](https://callrail.com) **`C`** - Reads and manages CallRail call tracking data and account configuration to analyze interactions and attribute results to marketing sources. *Use case: Reviewing which campaigns drove inbound calls last week, checking a tracking number's routing setup, summarizing call outcomes by source.*

- [Conjointly](https://conjointly.com) **`C`** - Lists survey research experiments, reads reports, segments and analysis results, checks respondent quality and sloppy answers, reads simulations, starts analyses, and requests data exports, without changing experiments or respondent data. *Use case: Pulling the preference shares from a pricing conjoint study, flagging respondents with suspicious completion times, exporting raw answers for a brand tracker wave.*

- [Developer Distribution](https://docs.daily.dev/developer-distribution/) **`C`** - Shows which topics, formats and title patterns developers read more or less on daily.dev last week, reads weekly intel for a company page, and publishes posts to it. *Use case: Checking whether Rust posts gained reads last week before a launch, publishing a release post to a company squad.*
- [DimensionKit](https://dimensionkit.com) **`C`** - Creates, updates and publishes scored assessments with dimensions, questions, answer options and result tiers, checks readiness before publishing, and reads respondent submissions with scores and contact details. *Use case: Building a marketing-maturity scorecard with five dimensions, publishing it after a readiness check, reviewing the scores of last week's respondents.*
- [Distribution Engineer](https://www.distribution.engineer) **`C`** - Reads and changes a Distribution Engineer workspace, including the offer, posts and their results, tracked links and booking hours, and prepares and schedules posts with tracked links, with changes made only after approval. *Use case: Scheduling a YouTube short with a tracked link for Friday, seeing which post brought last week's two sales.*
- [Distrove](https://distrove.app) **`C`** - Reads a business's website with Distrove to produce a growth report on the best channels to get customers, and checks its competitors and the ads they run. *Use case: Getting a marketing plan for a local plumbing company, seeing which ads a rival bakery runs.*

- [Reqlick](https://reqlick.com) **`C`** - Creates and manages Reqlick short links, QR codes and file links, and returns click counts, breakdowns, top links and click exports. *Use case: Creating a short link with UTM tags for a newsletter and checking its clicks by country a week later.*
- [Reviewflowz: Customer Reviews](https://www.reviewflowz.com) **`C`** - Reads customer reviews collected by Reviewflowz from many platforms, analyzes sentiment, compares profiles, runs reports, drafts replies and tags and assigns reviews. *Use case: Comparing Trustpilot and G2 sentiment this quarter and drafting replies to new negative reviews.*
- [REVIEWS.AI](https://www.reviews.ai) **`C`** - Reads aggregated customer reviews from monitored products and locations, returning sentiment summaries, ratings, NPS, star distributions, topics, extracts and review text, and exports filtered reviews to CSV. *Use case: Pulling this month's one-star reviews for a product, comparing complaint themes between two quarters, exporting Amazon reviews to CSV.*
- [Roivenue](https://www.roivenue.com) **`C`** - Answers questions about Roivenue attribution data — channel and campaign revenue, cost, ROMI, conversions, customer journeys — and reports data-source freshness, read-only. *Use case: Checking how paid search performed under a data-driven attribution model, tracing the touchpoints behind one order, confirming when a source last refreshed.*
- [Rus.sh](https://rus.sh) **`C`** - Creates, updates, pauses and archives Rus.sh short links and QR codes, and returns link and QR code stats and account stats. *Use case: Creating a short link with a custom address for a flyer and checking its clicks by device.*
- [Scanlo](https://scanlo.io) **`C`** - Creates tracked QR codes and multi-link hubs for small service businesses, repoints or switches off existing codes, and reports scan counts by code and placement and hub button taps. *Use case: Creating a Google reviews QR code for a front desk, repointing a window booking code to a new scheduler, comparing scans between counter and window placements.*
- [SegMetrics](https://segmetrics.io) **`C`** - Queries marketing attribution data, funnel conversion rates, contact segments, and top traffic sources across products, tags, lists, and campaigns. *Use case: Finding which traffic source drives the most revenue, comparing funnel conversion by campaign, segmenting leads by purchase history.*
- [Signal by StackEnhance](https://stackenhance.com) **`C`** - Reports how often ecommerce brands send marketing email, drawing on an archive of more than 1,000 online stores mostly in Sweden, Norway, Denmark, Finland and Germany, with brand summaries and links to their campaign pages. *Use case: Checking how many emails a competing fashion brand sent last month, finding which brands in Sweden email most often.*
- [Simple Click Tracker](https://simpleclicktracker.com) **`C`** - Finds, creates, updates and, after confirmation, deletes tracked short links on custom domains in Simple Click Tracker, filters by domain, group or link type, and ranks links by clicks, for owners or admins on a plan that includes integrations. *Use case: Creating a tracked link for a webinar replay, finding the five most-clicked affiliate links by unique clicks.*
- [Smacient](https://smacient.com/products/marketing-context-claude) **`C`** - Marketing data and competitive intelligence - analytics, ads, social, marketplaces, and app stores - straight into Claude. *Use case: Checking how a competitor's app store ranking moved after their last update, comparing social engagement against a rival brand, pulling marketplace pricing data before adjusting a listing.*
- [Snipzr](https://www.snipzr.com) **`C`** - Creates branded short links singly or up to 100 at once, builds UTM campaign links, generates QR codes with custom colors and a center logo, and reports daily clicks, QR scans and campaign rollups with bot traffic counted separately. *Use case: Shortening 40 event links on a branded domain for a conference, making a logo-centered QR code for a flyer, comparing clicks by campaign after a newsletter.*
- [Songstats](https://developers.stats.company/mcp) **`C`** - Returns read-only music analytics for artists, labels, collaborators and tracks, comparing performance over time, audience geography, playlist and curator drivers and release momentum across supported music platforms, with catalog search and Enterprise API endpoint access. *Use case: Comparing an artist's performance before and after a new single, finding which playlists drove a track's growth, mapping where a label's audience is concentrated.*
- [Source](https://source.app) **`C`** - Collects business data such as page visits, calendar bookings, sales funnel stages and payments under a single contact ID, answers questions and runs SQL over it, and lists tracked websites and describes the available data. *Use case: Tracing which first-visit pages led to paying customers, counting bookings that converted to payments last month.*
- [SparkToro](https://sparktoro.com) **`C`** - Creates SparkToro audience reports from a plain-language description and reads the audience's websites, podcasts, social accounts, YouTube channels, Reddit, press, search keywords, demographics and market size. *Use case: Finding podcasts and subreddits that home bakers follow, sizing the audience of B2B SaaS founders in the US.*
- [Sporely](https://sporely.io) **`C`** - Reads the Sporely growth board for the signed-in brand, with HubSpot funnel stages week by week against their usual range and logged marketing actions with results and verdicts. *Use case: Checking whether a spring paid campaign moved MQLs, reviewing which events last quarter were marked as working.*
- [Strateego](https://strateego.ai) **`C`** - Reads competitive intelligence from Strateego, including competitors' top social posts, recent ads, new moves, brand positioning, Google reputation and semantic search across their content. *Use case: Reviewing competitors' latest Meta ads, comparing Google review ratings, summarizing a rival's new product moves this month.*
- [Supercurve Marketing Teammate](https://supercurve.ai) **`C`** - Reads paid ad performance across Google, Meta, LinkedIn, Bing and ChatGPT Ads, competitor research, AI-search visibility, analytics, knowledge base, inbox and meeting notes from Supercurve, tracks competitors and AI-search prompts, sets LinkedIn content pillars and drafts posts and blog articles for approval. *Use case: Checking which prompts mention a brand in AI answers, adding a new competitor to tracking, drafting a LinkedIn post from a short brief.*
- [SurveyMethods EU](https://surveymethods.com) **`C`** - Creates SurveyMethods surveys on the UK and Europe site, makes share links, lists surveys and responses and summarizes results. *Use case: Creating a 10-question event feedback survey for a London conference, summarizing the responses.*
- [SurveyNinja](https://surveyninja.io) **`C`** - Builds SurveyNinja surveys from a description or template, applies themes, variables and hidden options, manages folders, tags and promocodes, publishes surveys and reads responses and reports. *Use case: Building an NPS survey for an online shop, counting answers to the delivery question, publishing it Monday.*
- [SurvIQ](https://surviqapp.com) **`C`** - Creates, updates, publishes, pauses and closes SurvIQ surveys, forms, quizzes and availability polls, and reads responses, summaries and cross-tab results. *Use case: Building a customer satisfaction survey, cross-tabbing satisfaction by age group after a week.*
- [swarmie](https://swarmie.harshpatadia.com) **`C`** - Starts Swarmie reviews of a startup pitch by a panel of AI reviewers, lists review types, reads the decision memo and asks individual reviewers about their reactions. *Use case: Running a Validate review on a pitch for a school admin tool, asking the harshest reviewer why.*
- [Tag Docket](https://tagdocket.com) **`C`** - Creates UTM links and short codes from a client's approved Tag Docket values, checks links and URLs, finds and previews links, reads taxonomies and returns QR codes. *Use case: Minting UTM links for a client's spring campaign across three channels, checking a link before print.*
- [Tellz](https://tellz.ai) **`C`** - Delivers a daily brief of competitors' public Instagram posts, Facebook and Instagram ads, LinkedIn posts, news mentions and watched web pages, searches collected items, and proposes, confirms or undoes changes to followed competitors, sources and brief timing. *Use case: Pulling today's competitor brief, searching every offer a rival posted this month, adding a news source to watch.*
- [Tesemble](https://tesemble.com) **`C`** - Tests product briefs and outbound copy against a factorial grid of synthetic buyer personas, suggesting audiences and message variants, running ICP discovery, GTM validation and message hunts, comparing and forking runs, saving audiences and reading segment insights, with simulations spending account credits. *Use case: Testing three cold-email openers against synthetic CFO personas, running ICP discovery for a new analytics product, comparing two simulation runs by segment.*
- [ThankYou Analytics](https://thankyouanalytics.com) **`C`** - Reads channel performance, attribution model comparisons, positional contribution, channel interactions and device and browser breakdowns from a ThankYou Analytics account. *Use case: Comparing how paid social is credited under different attribution models, finding which channels act as closers, reviewing ROAS by channel last quarter.*
- [ThoughtMetric](https://thoughtmetric.io) **`C`** - Queries e-commerce marketing attribution across channels for Shopify, WooCommerce, BigCommerce, Magento and custom stores, with five attribution models and configurable lookback windows. *Use case: Comparing first-touch and last-touch revenue for a campaign, widening the lookback window to match a long buying cycle, checking which channel drove yesterday's orders.*
- [toui URL Shortener](https://toui.io) **`C`** - Creates short links with UTM channel tags, groups them into campaigns, reports clicks by day, country, referrer and device per link or campaign, and edits link-in-bio page buttons. *Use case: Shortening a launch-post link tagged for a newsletter, comparing clicks by country across a spring campaign, adding a new product button to a link-in-bio page.*
- [Tracklution](https://www.tracklution.com) **`C`** - Runs traffic, event, session and conversion reports on Tracklution tracking containers, reads data quality insights, and guides server-side tracking installation and verification. *Use case: Finding which sources drove the most purchases in the last seven days, verifying tracking on a newly launched store.*
- [Trendata](https://trendata.io) **`C`** - Retrieves consumer search-volume totals and breakdowns by product category and location from Trendata, compares places and periods, and shows growth and forecasts where available. *Use case: Comparing search demand for a product category in two locations, checking whether interest in a snack category grew this year.*
- [TrendsAGI](https://trendsagi.com) **`C`** - Reports trending topics by category or brand with volume, velocity and trajectory, AI insights on themes, sentiment and audience, recommendations, crisis events and watchlists. *Use case: Checking whether a trend is still rising before committing ad budget, pulling marketing angles for a creative brief.*
- [Triggerbee](https://www.triggerbee.com) **`C`** - Reports onsite campaign conversions, signups, revenue and traffic, compares experiences across sites and periods, generates audiences from descriptions, and creates, edits, publishes or unpublishes popups, forms, surveys and personalization campaigns. *Use case: Comparing newsletter popup conversions across two market sites last month, creating an audience of returning visitors from a short description, updating a holiday campaign's targeting before launch.*
- [Two Minute Reports For Marketing Analytics](https://twominutereports.com) **`C`** - Analyze PPC, SEO, social, ecommerce & email marketing data from Facebook Ads (Meta), Google Ads, TikTok Ads, LinkedIn Ads, Google Analytics 4, Amazon Ads, Shopify and 25+ platforms in one place. *Use case: Comparing TikTok Ads and Facebook Ads performance side by side for the same campaign, checking Amazon Ads spend against Shopify sales, pulling an SEO summary alongside PPC data in one place.*
- [Uberall](https://uberall.com) **`C`** - Reads Uberall locations, listings, reviews and social posts, updates location details, replies to reviews, and reports impressions, clicks, listing health and visibility. *Use case: Finding stores with the slowest review response times, replying to a one-star review at a Leeds branch.*
- [Uneed](https://www.uneed.best) **`C`** - Searches Uneed's catalog of software products, shows profiles, reviews and alternatives, and lists trending launches, launch stats, deals and SaaS for sale. *Use case: Finding open-source alternatives to a screen recorder, browsing this week's top launches.*
- [Uniqode](https://www.uniqode.com) **`C`** - Reports Uniqode QR code and Linkpage performance — scans, unique visitors, link clicks, and country, device, and time breakdowns — and builds custom dashboards. *Use case: Checking which in-store QR codes are scanned most, comparing two campaigns by tag, building a dashboard of weekly scan trends.*
- [Upbuild](https://www.uptimal.com) **`C`** - Provides read-only queries across ten marketing and analytics platforms, including GA4, Google Ads, Meta Ads, TikTok Ads, Adobe Analytics, and Google Tag Manager. *Use case: Comparing spend efficiency between Meta and TikTok last month, auditing which tags fire on a checkout page, pulling a GA4 channel report for a review.*
- [UTMKit](https://utmkit.co) **`C`** - Creates and updates UTM-tagged short links under workspace naming rules, organizes campaigns and tags, returns QR codes and click reports, finds broken links and publishes read-only client report links. *Use case: Building tagged links for a newsletter campaign, finding links whose destinations broke, sharing a click report with a client.*
- [Varify MCP](https://varify.io) **`C`** - Varify's MCP connector lets Claude create, manage, and pull results for website A/B testing experiments through natural language. *Use case: Ask Claude to set up a 50/50 split test on a pricing page and later pull the revenue results for the last seven days.*
- [Verso](https://www.askverso.ai) **`C`** - Reads qualitative research studies, briefs, interview guides, reports and participant answers, shows theme breakdowns, queries personas, designs and changes studies, sets study status and exports results. *Use case: Pulling participant quotes on packaging, splitting a theme by age group for a slide.*
- [Vibewatch](https://vibewatch.io) **`C`** - Reads web3 community sentiment from Discord, Telegram, X, Bluesky and other platforms, including vibe scores by platform, sentiment trends, daily highlights and lowlights, weekly reports, message search and market context. *Use case: Explaining a sentiment dip on Discord last week, summarizing the latest weekly community report, searching negative messages about a token launch.*
- [Visual Quiz Builder](https://www.visualquizbuilder.com) **`C`** - Reads Shopify product quiz structures, responses, metrics and customer insights, plus chat agent metrics and store details, so quiz performance can be analyzed in conversation. *Use case: Comparing a skin quiz's completion rate this month with last month, checking how a store's chat agent is performing, summarizing what customers answered most often.*
- [Voxolib](https://www.voxolib.com) **`C`** - Reads VoxoLib call statistics, listing tracking numbers and giving WebCallback and Call Tracking call volumes by status for a chosen period. *Use case: Checking how many tracked calls were missed yesterday on one tracking number.*
- [Voyado Customer Engagement](https://voyado.com) - Finds and queries Voyado Customer Engagement data to analyze performance and build summaries and dashboards, and sends improvement suggestions to Voyado. *Use case: Building a dashboard of last quarter's customer engagement performance, sending Voyado a suggestion for a missing metric.*
- [VTurb](https://www.vturb.com) **`C`** - Reads video sales letter analytics such as plays, conversions, retention curves, traffic and UTM breakdowns, heatmaps and benchmarks, creates, starts and manages A/B tests and traffic splits, updates player controls, CTAs, overlays, pixels and autoplay, and imports, uploads, duplicates and organizes videos and folders, with every change previewed and applied only after confirmation. *Use case: Ranking VSLs by conversions this month, checking which A/B test variant is most likely to win, adding a timed CTA button at the pitch moment.*
- [Weavely](https://www.weavely.io) **`C`** - Runs validated, read-only SQL against a marketing data warehouse in Google BigQuery that syncs ad, analytics and ecommerce data from platforms such as Google Ads, Meta Ads, Google Analytics 4, LinkedIn Ads, Microsoft Ads and Shopify, with costly queries run only after confirmation, schema lookups, client context documents and analyst playbooks for pacing, anomaly checks and cross-platform reviews, on a Weavely subscription. *Use case: Comparing Meta and Google Ads cost per conversion for one client last month, running a pacing check against budget, summarizing GA4 sessions by channel.*
- [Webz.io Blogs Segmentation](https://docs.webz.io/docs/webz/blogs-api-segmentation-mcp) **`C`** - Counts blog coverage of a topic in aggregation buckets by source site, author, organization and other supported fields, returning counts rather than posts or excerpts. *Use case: Ranking which blog sites covered an electric-vehicle launch most, identifying the authors writing most often about a competitor.*
- [Whatagraph](https://whatagraph.com) **`C`** - Query your cross-channel marketing data from 60+ sources — Google Ads, Meta, GA4, Shopify, and more — using natural language. *Use case: Comparing Google Ads spend against Meta spend for the same week, checking a GA4 conversion drop, pulling Shopify revenue by channel for a monthly report.*
- [Whooshly](https://whooshly.co) **`C`** - Creates short links, dynamic QR codes, UTM templates, digital business cards, link-in-bio pages, and lead-capture funnels, and retrieves link and QR analytics. *Use case: Generating a QR code for a print flyer, building UTM links for a newsletter, redirecting a live campaign link to a new page.*
- [Wingify](https://wingify.com) - Optimize digital experiences directly from Claude and supercharge your CRO workflow. *Use case: Reviewing which digital experience is converting best, adjusting the workflow before scaling it to more traffic.*
- [www.trendsmcp.ai](https://www.trendsmcp.ai) **`C`** - Returns live and historical trend data for keywords across search, video, social, shopping, developer package, entertainment and news sources, including growth, time series, top trends and top posts. *Use case: Comparing five-year search growth for two product names, checking what is trending on TikTok today.*
- [XICMO](https://xicmo.com) **`C`** - Reads website marketing data, including SEO, AI search visibility and traffic scores, SEO audit issues, ranked action items, recent agent runs, content drafts and credits, and answers strategy questions from workspace history. *Use case: Reviewing the top-ranked action items this week, checking which SEO audit issues remain open, reading the latest blog and LinkedIn drafts.*
- [Yogi](https://www.meetyogi.com) **`C`** - AI-powered consumer insights platform that analyzes product reviews, support tickets, and social feedback for CPG brands. *Use case: Ask which product attributes are driving negative sentiment in recent customer reviews.*
- [YPulse Insights](https://www.ypulse.com) **`C`** - Gen Z and Millennial consumer insights from YPulse, directly in Claude. *Use case: Checking how Gen Z shopping habits differ from Millennials before finalizing a campaign brief.*

## Sales Prospecting and Engagement

- [1up](https://1up.ai) **`C`** - Answers questions from a company's knowledge sources with citations, works RFPs, DDQs and security questionnaires by editing, assigning, commenting on, approving and exporting answers, runs bulk actions, and manages the Q&A library, tags and imported content. *Use case: Drafting answers to a 200-question security questionnaire, assigning pricing questions to a sales engineer, saving corrected answers to the Q&A library.*
- [6sense](https://6sense.com) **`C`** - Retrieves 6sense account data, 6QA trends, keyword activity and campaign performance from natural-language questions, and runs market research and sales news lookups. *Use case: Checking an account's 6QA trend, reviewing keyword activity and campaign performance for an account, pulling sales news on a company.*
- [Actively](https://www.actively.ai) - AI agents that work every account across the revenue funnel. *Use case: Pipeline generation, deal execution, account prioritization, GTM sales intelligence.*
- [AdaptClose](https://adaptclose.com) **`C`** - Reads scored sales-call recordings and transcripts, searches transcripts, uploads and commits new recordings, manages scoring playbooks and checklists, and records door-knocking outcomes, asking before any change by default. *Use case: Pulling every call that scored under 60 last week, updating the scorecard to weight objection handling higher.*
- [AdvertiserLeads](https://advertiserleads.com) **`C`** - Lists US ecommerce brands flagged as newly advertising, scaling, struggling or stopping on Meta by niche, looks up any brand's Meta ad activity, and checks a store's platform and tracking pixels. *Use case: Finding skincare brands that started Meta ads this week for agency outreach, checking whether a Shopify store also runs a TikTok pixel.*
- [Agentibus](https://agentibus.ai) **`C`** - Lists leads and conversations of an AI sales agent on Instagram and WhatsApp, approves or discards drafted replies, pauses or resumes the agent or takes over a single lead, and adds web pages or text to its knowledge. *Use case: Reviewing drafted replies to new Instagram leads before they go out, taking over a high-value lead by hand, adding an updated price list to the agent's knowledge.*
- [Airspeed](https://www.goairspeed.com) **`C`** - Finds customer calls and reads their summaries, transcripts, extracted insights, participants, and linked CRM identifiers, and can start agent conversations where enabled. *Use case: Preparing for a renewal meeting from past calls, pulling open questions a customer raised, reviewing commitments made on a recent call.*
- [Aligned](https://alignedup.com) **`C`** - Queries Aligned deal rooms for pipeline, engagement, and stakeholder activity, and creates rooms, adds sections, and updates mutual action plans after confirmation. *Use case: Pulling a pre-call brief on a stalled account, spinning up a room from a template for a new deal, adding next steps to a mutual action plan.*
- [Allegrow](https://www.allegrow.co) **`C`** - Improves outbound email deliverability with Allegrow: verifying contacts, warming inboxes, reporting spam rates and sender health, testing content, and searching connected mailboxes. *Use case: Cleaning a prospect list before a sequence, checking which rep's inbox is landing in spam, comparing two subject lines for primary-inbox placement.*
- [Alto](https://alto.video) **`C`** - Uploads finished videos and creates one tracked link per recipient, then reports who watched, how far, which chapters were reached, the most replayed parts, and where viewers dropped off. *Use case: Sending a demo video to three prospects with separate links, checking whether an investor watched an update, finding the part of a walkthrough people replayed most.*
- [Amdahl](https://amdahl.ai) **`C`** - Optimizes and evaluates outbound email and LinkedIn drafts against a company's own sales calls, notes and CRM data, researches buyers, and manages agents, evaluations and data connections. *Use case: Rewriting a cold email in a rep's voice, grading a LinkedIn message against past call themes, researching what a target account's buyers care about.*
- [Amotions AI](https://amotionsinc.com) **`C`** - Lists recent sales calls recorded in Amotions AI and reads their scores, coaching feedback, transcripts and coaching trends, read-only. *Use case: Reviewing the scores of the last five calls, drafting a follow-up email from this morning's call with a prospect, spotting objections across this week's calls.*
- [anVendor](https://anvendor.com) **`C`** - Finds companies that use a given SaaS product or business service by location, industry and size, checks account lists for a vendor after a cost quote is approved, and lists a company's services with estimated adoption and annual spend. *Use case: Finding mid-size logistics firms in Texas that use a competitor's payroll software, checking which accounts on a target list run a specific help desk tool, estimating a prospect's annual software spend.*
- [Apartee](https://apartee.app) **`C`** - Searches a sales team's approved Apartee answers and documents on product, security and pricing, and lists battlecards and open questions, each answer with its source, read-only. *Use case: Pulling the approved answer on SOC 2 status for a security questionnaire, checking the battlecard against a competitor before a demo.*
- [Apollo.io](https://www.apollo.io) - Searches Apollo people and companies by title, seniority, industry, technology, funding and hiring signals, pulls job postings, enriches people and companies, with phone number reveals using extra credits, saves and updates contacts, and adds contacts to sequences with confirmation or removes them. *Use case: Finding senior people at companies using a specific technology, enriching a list of people with work emails, adding new contacts to an existing sequence.*
- [Ask Nathan](https://asknathan.ai) **`C`** - Finds warm introduction paths into target accounts from a team's CRM, calendar and contact relationships with a 0 to 100 Path Confidence score and evidence, maps paths for pipeline deals and events, and after confirmation requests intros, logs interactions, merges duplicate people and imports people lists. *Use case: Finding who on the team can introduce a rep to a target account, checking the warmest path into each open deal, logging today's meetings.*
- [ASPR AI](https://www.aspr.ai) **`C`** - Prepares and recaps sales meetings with transcripts, MEDDPICC and sentiment extraction, suggested follow-up emails, pipeline exploration, and per-meeting coaching. *Use case: Prepping for tomorrow's discovery call, pulling follow-up email suggestions from yesterday's recap, reviewing coaching tips across last week's meetings.*
- [Attention](https://www.attention.com) - Searches call transcripts across a team in Attention, surfaces objections and trends, reviews scorecards and deal health, and creates shareable snippets from calls. *Use case: Finding the objections raised across a team's calls, reviewing a rep's scorecards before a coaching session, clipping a shareable snippet from a client call.*
- [AutoRFP.ai](https://autorfp.ai) - Search your RFP content library, check live project status, and draft with approved answers in Claude. *Use case: Finding the approved answer to a specific compliance question, checking which RFPs are due this week, drafting a response ahead of a Friday deadline.*
- [Badger Maps](https://www.badgermapping.com) **`C`** - Is the #1 field sales platform. Listed as separate Standard and Advanced connector tiers in the catalog. *Use case: Managing field sales operations.*
- [BIDALTO](https://bidalto.com) **`C`** - Searches open public tenders in Spain by sector, keyword, province and contract type, opens the official notice and estimates an offer price and margin. *Use case: Finding cleaning contracts in Valencia province, estimating the price for a 100,000 euro tender while keeping a 6 percent margin.*
- [Bidaya](https://bidaya.ai) **`C`** - Searches RFPs, tenders and firm knowledge for architecture, engineering and construction teams, including past proposals, resumes and project experience, summarizes pursuits and updates pursuit stages. *Use case: Finding open RFPs for a hospital project, searching past proposals for similar work, moving a pursuit to shortlisted.*
- [Bigmind](https://bigmind.ai) **`C`** - Turn meetings, CRM, pipeline, and company knowledge into answers and actions in Claude. *Use case: Checking what was decided in last Tuesday's meeting, pulling the current stage of a specific deal from the pipeline, answering a policy question from company documentation.*
- [BillionVerify](https://billionverify.com) **`C`** - Verifies single email addresses or batches of up to 50, returning validity, catch-all, disposable and role-account status with mail server checks, and reviews history, stats, async jobs and credits and manages webhooks. *Use case: Verifying a newsletter list before a send, checking whether a signup address is disposable, setting up a webhook for finished jobs.*
- [BirdSift](https://birdsift.com) **`C`** - Local business lead search over Google Places with website-quality flags, saved leads, CSV export, credit balance checks, and grounded cold outreach drafts, with confirmation before each credit-spending search or draft. *Use case: Searching one city and category for businesses with no mobile-friendly site, exporting the saved leads, drafting an outreach email from the verified findings.*
- [BizFetch](https://bizfetch.net) **`C`** - Scores Japanese companies, government subsidies and public tenders against user-defined conditions using gBizINFO, National Tax Agency corporate number and invoice data, EDINET filings and Japan Patent Office records, creates, tests and compares conditions, edits target lists, marks candidates, watches daily changes, stores a company profile and products, and sends results to kintone, Google Sheets or webhooks. *Use case: Scoring manufacturers in Aichi against a target-customer profile, checking which open subsidies fit the company's own products, sending shortlisted candidates to a Google Sheet.*
- [Bizu.ai](https://bizu.ai) **`C`** - Searches and summarizes a company's WhatsApp sales conversations, including unanswered customers, open requests, AI sentiment and risk readings and service metrics by seller, with links to each conversation. *Use case: Finding customers left without a reply, comparing response metrics by seller, reading what a customer asked for.*
- [BoothIQ](https://getboothiq.com) **`C`** - Reads trade show and event lead data, listing events with lead counts and captures by rep, searching leads by name, company or notes, and returning contact details, notes, conversation summaries, next steps and recording transcripts, read-only. *Use case: Listing leads who asked about pricing at a conference, seeing which rep captured the most leads at a show, reviewing next steps for today's booth conversations.*
- [BOSRAI](https://bosr.ai) **`C`** - Reads your BOSRAI sales pipeline, results, buying signals and prospect replies, and builds outreach sequences. *Use case: Reviewing this week's prospect replies, checking which accounts show buying signals, drafting a new outreach sequence.*
- [BounceWatch Signal Tracker](https://bouncewatch.com) **`C`** - Lists dated, sourced company signals such as funding rounds, executive moves, expansion and launches for tracked accounts, reads company briefs, pipeline and outreach history, tracks or untracks companies, sets categories and updates action-board items. *Use case: Catching up on what changed across tracked accounts this week, briefing on one company before a sales call, marking a follow-up action done.*
- [CallMarker](https://callmarker.com) **`C`** - Reads calls with outcomes, AI summaries and transcriptions, campaigns, customers, appointments, opportunities, products and users in a CallMarker sales and call-center account. *Use case: Reviewing which reps had the longest calls in the spring campaign yesterday, listing open opportunities with appointments this week.*
- [Caretta](https://www.caretta.so) **`C`** - Realtime AI assistant that helps sales reps answer technical product questions during live sales calls. *Use case: Surface product documentation and past call context to prepare for an upcoming technical sales call.*
- [Cargo](https://getcargo.ai) **`C`** - Operates a Cargo workspace: runs connector actions across 130+ integrations, enriches people and companies, queries CRM and revenue models, and reads team playbooks. *Use case: Enriching a list of inbound signups before routing them, checking the ICP definition a team already wrote, running one action across a batch of accounts.*
- [CiblePro](https://ciblepro.fr) **`C`** - Counts and previews French companies from the INSEE Sirene registry by activity, NAF code, département, creation date and sole-trader status, and finds the NAF code for a trade. *Use case: Counting electricians created in Gironde since January, finding the NAF code for bakeries.*
- [Claap](https://www.claap.io) - Searches Claap call recordings, summaries, and synced emails, and queries deals, companies, and contacts with their CRM fields and saved views. *Use case: Pulling next steps from yesterday's demo, finding every call where pricing came up as an objection, reviewing a MEDDIC view before a forecast meeting.*
- [Clay](https://www.clay.com) - Finds and enriches contacts at a company, a list of contacts or a company itself using Clay's contact databases and enrichment providers, adds contact and company data points, retrieves existing searches and checks remaining credits. *Use case: Finding VP-level finance leaders at a target company, enriching a list of contacts before outreach, checking remaining credits.*
